plan-2026-08-05T124709-6c4fac48/D-041 | 2026-08-06 | Select the best checkpoint by `val_box_iou` instead of `val_loss`, since `presence_loss` was 61.7% of val_loss while nearly image-independent.
plan-2026-08-05T124709-6c4fac48/D-047 | 2026-08-06 | Fix `num_boxes` to the conjunction `valid AND w>0 AND h>0`, restoring the validity flag a prior port-fidelity fix had silently dropped — a real shipped regression.
plan-2026-08-06T055747-1e650383/D-001 | 2026-08-06 | Probe first (pre-committed 3-arm gradient verdict), then ship deep supervision as repeated uniform blocks appended to the same packed prediction tensor (`[Q rows | presence row] x (1 + n_aux)`, main block first), rather than a new packed row axis or a second packed tensor.
plan-2026-08-06T055747-1e650383/D-004 | 2026-08-06 | Auxiliary decoder-layer blocks are summed into the total loss at the SAME weight as the main term — a plain sum, no per-layer coefficient or depth decay — because the reference applies none.
plan-2026-08-06T055747-1e650383/D-005 | 2026-08-06 | Auxiliary blocks' mask channels are zero-filled by the packer and the loss computes no mask term for any auxiliary block, matching the reference's `Masks` loss defaulting `compute_aux=False` while `Boxes`/`IABCEMdetr` default True.
plan-2026-08-06T055747-1e650383/D-006 | 2026-08-06 | `evaluate_sam3` branches on `model.num_aux_layers` and packs the auxiliary blocks too via `Sam3Image.call_per_layer`, so it never mis-slices the loss's packed-block-count contract while still performing exactly one forward pass per batch.
plan-2026-08-06T185813-fd80240f/D-005 | 2026-08-07 | Hard-code `anchor_size = 0.1776` — the seed-pooled mean of `sqrt(w*h)` over the train split's valid GT boxes — as `Sam3EncoderQuerySelection`'s default, kept distinct from `baselines.py`'s unrelated hand-written `GRID_BOX_SIZE = 0.2`.
plan-2026-08-06T185813-fd80240f/D-006 | 2026-08-07 | Selected proposal boxes enter the decoder detached (`stop_gradient`), and query content stays the learned `query_embed` table — the encoder head is supervised only by its own packed block, never by backprop through the decoder.
plan-2026-08-06T185813-fd80240f/D-007 | 2026-08-07 | The "which blocks get packed" rule lives in one module-level function, `select_prediction_blocks`, called by both `Sam3TrainingModel.call` and `evaluate_sam3`, so the evaluation path cannot silently drift from the training path again.
plan-2026-08-06T185813-fd80240f/D-009 | 2026-08-07 | Ship the connected-components detector as a first-class tested baseline member, excluded from `family_max` by an allowlist rather than a blocklist, so a pre-registered bar cannot be silently raised by a comparator that beats it.
plan-2026-08-07T065516-6add49a9/D-003 | 2026-08-07 | SUPPLEMENT the prompted-category filter with additive eval-only all-category record keys outside the training `signature`, never relax the filter, so every published `box_iou` stays comparable and zero RNG draws move.
plan-2026-08-07T065516-6add49a9/D-014 | 2026-08-07 | FiLM-modulate `memory` from the pooled prompt BEFORE both proposal MLPs (a bias on the objectness logits cannot change an argsort), created only under a default-OFF flag and deliberately NOT zero-initialized because this is a gate, not an anchor displacement.
plan-2026-08-07T065516-6add49a9/D-022 | 2026-08-07 | Fold the `--distractor-gap` and `--prompt-swap` results into the `--json` payload keyed per seed, writing into a COPY of the per-seed dict so `family_max`'s allowlist can never see them.
plan-2026-08-07T065516-6add49a9/D-023 | 2026-08-07 | Move the shared matched-IoU reduction and the distractor-target rule DOWN the dependency chain (`data.py` <- `train_sam3.py` <- `baselines.py`) rather than take a lazy import cycle or a third copy of the reduction.
plan-2026-08-07T174622-3b8002c3/D-001 | 2026-08-07 | Thread a defaulted val_seed_offset parameter through baselines.py only (never train_sam3.py::create_datasets), second offset 20_000, so a second independently-seeded val split needs zero published numbers to move.
plan-2026-08-07T174622-3b8002c3/D-003 | 2026-08-07 | Add one additive best_gap_model.keras ModelCheckpoint monitoring val_box_distractor_gap, proven training-neutral with its own control-first CPU A/B, leaving SELECTION_METRIC/EarlyStopping untouched.
plan-2026-08-10T130454-3649c19e/D-006 | 2026-08-10 | Delete the full DSv2 consumer closure (embedding_unet.py, lmunet.py, four trainers) rather than partially rewriting the two U-Nets to drop their downsampling paths.
plan-2026-08-10T130454-3649c19e/D-008 | 2026-08-10 | Keep the live, tested use_gate kwarg and its inert-sublayer pattern — D-007's premise (that the rewrite had already dropped it) measured false — and execute the DSv2 deletion D-005/D-006 actually required instead.
plan-2026-08-10T130454-3649c19e/D-010 | 2026-08-10 | Import the activation trio (_activation_spec/_resolve_activation/_serialize_activation) from clifford_block into clifford_rnn rather than re-copying a stale fork or extracting a new shared module.
plan-2026-08-10T130454-3649c19e/D-011 | 2026-08-10 | Delete the orphaned CliffordBottleneckMonitorCallback and its test rather than generalizing the surviving ConvUnextBottleneckMonitorCallback sibling to a model that never implements the contract it needs.
plan-2026-08-10T130454-3649c19e/D-012 | 2026-08-10 | Repoint test_weight_transfer.py's fixture to small functional Keras models sharing CliffordNetUNet's layer-naming convention, decoupling the utility's tests from any model package.
plan-2026-08-10T130454-3649c19e/D-014 | 2026-08-10 | Key memory_llm_custom_objects() by keras.saving.get_registered_name(cls), derived per class, rather than the bare class name or a hard-coded literal Custom> string.
plan-2026-08-10T130454-3649c19e/D-027 | 2026-08-10 | Scope the CliffordUNet architecture refusal to DenoiserPrior.from_pretrained's dynamic branch only, restoring the working fixed256 load that D-009 had wrongly refused outside any branch.
plan-2026-08-10T130454-3649c19e/D-029 | 2026-08-10 | Keep the hard private-Keras-import tripwire (no try/except fallback) for DropoutRNNCell, since a fallback cannot preserve the dropout-mask lifecycle it would silently lose.
plan-2026-08-10T183739-b007f435/D-003 | 2026-08-11 | Declare pad_token_id ADVISORY (never auto-derive attention_mask from it); document the caller-must-supply-mask contract and guard it with a masked-vs-unmasked forward-differs test.
plan-2026-08-10T183739-b007f435/D-006 | 2026-08-11 | Validate position_embedding_type against a hard-coded enum in BertEmbeddings.__init__ and raise ValueError, rather than a silent else-fallback like the normalization_type defect this plan removes.
plan-2026-08-10T183739-b007f435/D-007 | 2026-08-11 | Add the two new conditional sub-layer constructions IN PLACE, without reordering existing statements, to preserve the RNG-seeded weight-initialization order the I-1 bit-identity requirement depends on.
plan-2026-08-10T183739-b007f435/D-008 | 2026-08-11 | Compute the sinusoidal position table in float32 unconditionally, then cast to the dtype of the tensor it is actually summed with — fixes the mixed_float16 crash without a dtype-only fix that fails to survive the layer boundary.
plan-2026-08-10T183739-b007f435/D-009 | 2026-08-11 | Hoist normalization_type and position_embedding_type enums to module-level constants shared by the constructor and the factory validator, removing a hand-maintained lockstep duplication.
plan-2026-08-10T183739-b007f435/D-010 | 2026-08-11 | Move type_vocab_size to optional (default None) with a conditional-required rule in validate_embedding_config keyed off the registry's own default, not a hard-coded True.
plan-2026-08-10T183739-b007f435/D-011 | 2026-08-11 | Pass every kwarg explicitly at the create_embedding_layer call site, including four that look redundant, each proven load-bearing by mutation — factory defaults are BERT's, not neutral.
plan-2026-08-10T183739-b007f435/D-012 | 2026-08-11 | Document, do not fix, that from_variant(pretrained=<path>) raises on every route (unbuilt: wrong dummy-input dtype; built: unsupported by_name kwarg); keras.models.load_model(path) is the working route.
plan-2026-08-10T183739-b007f435/D-013 | 2026-08-11 | Assert STRUCTURE (mask_zero flags, traceback origin, built sub-layer class/epsilon, post-forward weight paths) rather than forward output where the shipped behaviour is numerically invisible, and treat in-test assertion ORDER as load-bearing.
plan-2026-08-10T183739-b007f435/D-015 | 2026-08-11 | Normalize position_ids rank once in BertEmbeddings.call for both branches (broadcast rank-1, raise on unsupported rank), removing the asymmetry where the sinusoidal branch crashed on inputs the learned branch accepted.
plan-2026-08-10T183739-b007f435/D-016 | 2026-08-11 | Compute the sinusoidal table in the wider of float32/variable_dtype (gated on variable_dtype, never compute_dtype) so a float64 policy gets float64-grade precision, not just float64 storage.
plan-2026-08-10T183739-b007f435/D-017 | 2026-08-11 | Anchor the sinusoidal/rank test file's value-varying assertions against the measured M28 vacuity (a shape+finiteness check that stayed green under a silent position clamp).
plan-2026-08-10T183739-b007f435/D-018 | 2026-08-11 | Correct the mask_zero rationale to the measured fact — it is inert downstream, no _keras_mask survives the embedding sum at either setting — after the reviewer showed D-011's original two-masks-collide hazard claim was false.
plan-2026-08-10T183739-b007f435/D-021 | 2026-08-11 | Materialize non-tensor position_ids via hasattr/convert_to_tensor before reading rank, fixing a regression D-015 itself introduced (list/tuple/int inputs raised an unnamed AttributeError on both branches).
plan-2026-08-11T012340-f63796dc/D-001 | 2026-08-11 | Build one new standalone BeitAttention layer registered as a 'beit' attention type, compose BeitModel from TransformerLayer with no signature change, and mirror models/energy_transformer/ file-for-file for the backbone/MIM/classifier split.

plan-2026-08-11T012340-f63796dc/D-006 | 2026-08-11 | Generalize validate_attention_config's positive-value loop to compare a sequence value componentwise (a scalar wrapped in a 1-tuple), keyed on the parameter name exactly as before, so a tuple window_size validates at every door.

plan-2026-08-11T012340-f63796dc/D-011 | 2026-08-11 | Keep BeitAttention's relative-position index as a numpy int32 array computed in build() and convert it to a tensor inside call(), so fit() on an unbuilt model no longer raises InaccessibleTensorError.

plan-2026-08-11T110821-54118fdd/D-001 | 2026-08-11 | Add an explicit input_mode: Literal["image","sequence"] constructor kwarg; sequence mode accepts both rank-3 and rank-4 by expanding/squeezing at the top/end of call() so the internal 4-D weight layout is unchanged; causal=True implies input_mode="sequence"; the three external call sites are not migrated.

plan-2026-08-11T110821-54118fdd/D-008 | 2026-08-11 | Split mixed-precision test coverage by strength: smoke guards for finite/dtype/shape at block level (which do not discriminate the D-005 fix), plus one isolated-reduction test asserting relative error against a float64 reference tolerance set to the worst value across devices, not the headline number.
plan-2026-08-11T110821-54118fdd/D-009 | 2026-08-11 | Prove the two already-correct characterization claims (wedge/ctx_mode inertness, activations-are-applied) exclusively by dead-component/behaviour-swap injection rather than by revert, using a shared twin-builder that verifies elementwise weight equality before any output comparison, with layer_scale_init=1.0 (never the layer's own 1e-5 default) to avoid a vacuous float32 comparison.

plan-2026-08-11T201945-91938f65/D-001 | 2026-08-11 | Wire FNet's use_stochastic_depth/stochastic_depth_rate knob by adding two independent StochasticDepth instances to FNetEncoderBlock (one per residual branch) and forwarding a linear_drop_path_rates schedule from the model, imitating transformer.py's majority two-instance pattern.

plan-2026-08-14T135600-mcsplit/D-002 | 2026-08-14 | Populate `models/mobile_clip/__init__.py` with a curated `__all__` exporting both models' classes and factories, but DELIBERATELY NOT `MODEL_VARIANTS`: v1's is the class attribute `MobileClipModel.MODEL_VARIANTS` (`b`/`s0`/`s1`/`s2`) and v2's is module-level (`mobileclip2_s*`), with disjoint keys and with `mci0`/`mci1`/`mci2` meaning real MCi rows in v2 and `keras.applications` stand-ins in v1. A package-level re-export would make that collision invisible.
plan-2026-08-14T135600-mcsplit/D-003 | 2026-08-14 | Merge the two READMEs rather than keeping a v1-only and a v2-only document: `models/mobile_clip/README.md` covers both generations with an explicit v1-vs-v2 table and a §17 "which should I use", and `models/fastvit/README.md` is new and owns the MCi tower + deviations X-1/X-2/X-3/X-5 in full (mobile_clip summarizes and links). Also REPLACED v1's `faghri2023mobileclip` BibTeX entry, whose author list, title, venue and year were all fabricated, with the real Vasu et al. CVPR 2024 citation.
plan-2026-08-14T160000-mcv2shape/D-001 | 2026-08-14 | Give `MobileClipV2Model` v1's API SHAPE: `MODEL_VARIANTS` moves to a nested CLASS attribute (`embed_dim` + `image_config` + `text_config`, sub-dicts being literal encoder kwargs), `__init__` collapses 18 flat kwargs to v1's `(embed_dim, image_config, text_config, logit_scale_init, output_dict, ...)`, and `output_dict` + `summary()` are adopted. Variant KEY NAMES stay (`mobileclip2_s0`...) — they are provenance-bearing, one row per supplied JSON config. AMENDS plan-2026-08-14T135600-mcsplit/D-002, whose rationale (v1 class-attribute vs v2 module-level) this retires: both tables are now class attributes, so the collision it guarded against is gone and the remaining hazards are the NAMING ones (`text_config['embed_dim']` is the text width; `image_config['variant']` is not the row key).
plan-2026-08-14T160000-mcv2shape/D-002 | 2026-08-14 | KEEP the nine v2 behaviours v1 lacks, against the pull to adopt v1's method list wholesale: tower injection + explicit tower serialization, per-tower `built` guards in `build()`, `get_build_config`/`build_from_config`, `compute_output_shape`, `compute_logit_scale`, `_validate_config`, `logit_scale_max`, the tower-name constants, and `logit_scale` created in `build()` not `__init__`. Each prevents a specific measured failure. ADD read-only `@property` accessors over the sub-dicts (surface v1 lacks) so validation, `summary()` and ~15 assertions do not become hand-written two-level dict lookups.
plan-2026-08-14T160000-mcv2shape/D-003 | 2026-08-14 | `output_dict=False` returns a 5-TUPLE `(image_features, text_features, logits_per_image, logits_per_text, logit_scale)`, NOT v1's 3-tuple, which would silently drop both logits matrices; `compute_output_shape` branches on the same flag. *`[PRE-MANIFEST]` block appended 2026-08-14 by `plan-2026-08-14T183218-f4c612aa` step 8, implementing ruling `plan-2026-08-14T163016-b7395570`/D-009. Each line names an anchor id referenced under `src/dl_techniques/models/` that resolves to NOTHING: absent from this manifest, its plan directory gone from `plans/`, and no `## <plan-id>` section in `plans/DECISIONS.md`. A stub carries no rationale — that is the whole point: a reader who greps such an id now gets "known-lost" rather than silence, and silence and known-lost are different answers. Stubs are NOT decision records; do not read one as evidence a choice was made here.*
*Re-derive the set (repo root): `grep -rhoE '[Pp]lan[_-][0-9]{4}-[0-9]{2}-[0-9]{2}[A-Za-z_0-9-]*/D-[0-9]+' src/dl_techniques/models/ | sort -u`, then subtract (a) ids already matching `^[A-Za-z0-9_.-]+/D-[0-9]+` in this file, (b) ids whose `plans/<plan-id>/` directory still exists, (c) ids with a `## <plan-id>` section in `plans/DECISIONS.md`. That pipeline measured 104 ids at append time; 95 of them are also flagged `[anchor-unknown-plan]` by `validate-plan.mjs`, the other 9 being references that are not literal `# DECISION` comments in a scanned source file.*

plan-2026-08-14T183218-f4c612aa/D-006 | 2026-08-14 | Flip DiffusionScheduler's default prediction_type to 'sample' (docstring states it as a deliberate choice) rather than rewriting call()'s supervision target to noise, since all three shipped nano_vlm_world_model presets only reach it via the class default.
plan-2026-08-14T183218-f4c612aa/D-007 | 2026-08-14 | Default create_modern_nanovlm's fusion_strategy to 'cross_attention' and add a named equal-sequence-length ValueError guard at the top of _call_tensor_fusion, replacing an opaque framework InvalidArgumentError.
plan-2026-08-14T183218-f4c612aa/D-008 | 2026-08-14 | Add attention_type: str = 'multi_head' to TiRexCore.__init__, thread it into block construction and get_config(), leaving attention_args wired unconditionally rather than branching by type.
plan-2026-08-14T183218-f4c612aa/D-009 | 2026-08-14 | Add an opt-in return_tokens flag so RELGTTransformerBlock.call can return the local token sequence alongside its summary, and chain that token sequence (not the collapsed summary, not the fixed seed) across blocks in RELGT.call.
plan-2026-08-14T183218-f4c612aa/D-010 | 2026-08-14 | Resolve UniversalInvertedBottleneck's stride onto the first spatial operator that exists via a derived precedence (dw1 -> dw2 -> project_conv) instead of hardcoding it on dw1 alone, fixing a silent compute_output_shape mismatch.
plan-2026-08-14T183218-f4c612aa/D-011 | 2026-08-14 | Add a genuinely new pre-expansion start_dw slot to UniversalInvertedBottleneck (head of the stride precedence) and map block_type to which depthwise POSITIONS are occupied, making the four block types paper-faithful structures.
plan-2026-08-14T183218-f4c612aa/D-012 | 2026-08-14 | Derive BLT patch ids as the count of entropy-threshold crossings at positions <= t, saturated at max_patches-1, so causality and the row-sum-to-seq_len invariant hold by construction rather than by top-k-by-magnitude ranking.
plan-2026-08-14T183218-f4c612aa/D-014 | 2026-08-15 | Chain RELGTTransformerBlock's global/centroid summary into the returned token sequence too (additive broadcast), closing a 34-of-107 gradient-dead-parameter defect the token-only chaining (D-009) left behind.
plan-2026-08-14T183218-f4c612aa/D-016 | 2026-08-15 | Delete generate_from_text's x_0->eps conversion before scheduler.step(), since step()'s 'sample' branch already consumes x_0 directly; the two sites' opposite conventions were driving the reverse diffusion process with the wrong quantity at every step.
plan-2026-08-14T183218-f4c612aa/D-017 | 2026-08-15 | Scale RELGTTransformerBlock's broadcast summary by a fixed SUMMARY_BROADCAST_SCALE=0.1 so the token-invariant component (RMS 1.0 by LayerNorm construction) stops swamping the token-varying signal, which was compounding with depth; a learnable scale was rejected because the last block's output is discarded, making it provably gradient-dead.
plan-2026-08-14T183218-f4c612aa/D-018 | 2026-08-15 | Replace DynamicPatcher's vocabulary-based degeneracy warning (fired on 100% of shipped configurations) with warn_if_segmentation_is_degenerate(entropy), an opt-in method keyed on the observed boundary rate, called once by the trainer after entropy-model pretraining.
plan-2026-08-14T183218-f4c612aa/D-024 | 2026-08-15 | Add an optional mask parameter to warn_if_segmentation_is_degenerate so right-padding (50-87.5% of probed positions on shipped presets) can no longer dilute the observed boundary rate below the degenerate-arm threshold.

Its in-source anchor existed ONLY to explain the asymmetry with D-014, so it was deleted alongside it in `df0bb5977` (D-004b of `plan-2026-09-01T110541-dcc1574a`: a cross-reference that exists only to explain a flag goes when the flag goes). The RULING still holds and is now unremarkable — after the flag removal there is no asymmetry left to explain, and no site under `src/` passes `legacy_alias=False`.
plan-2026-08-25T053412-0f1fa04f/D-002 | 2026-08-25 | The grid/zigzag short-circuit must be proven OUTPUT-IDENTICAL via a `git show HEAD`-golden, RED-proved harness before any restructuring edit, not merely assumed inert.
plan-2026-08-25T053412-0f1fa04f/D-006 | 2026-08-25 | Build the `(W**2,W**2)` relative-position index in `build()` from a numpy `@staticmethod`, not `__init__` or `add_weight`+`.assign()`, cutting construction from 5.639 GB to the 0.625 GB floor.
plan-2026-08-25T053412-0f1fa04f/D-007 | 2026-08-25 | Accept the step-3 short-circuit and re-pin the 6 ragged harness cells against a pad-masked reference instead of the raw golden, because the OLD code leaked unmasked padding into the softmax and was wrong, not merely slow.
plan-2026-08-25T053412-0f1fa04f/D-010 | 2026-08-25 | `partition_mode='band'` refuses `use_relative_position_bias=True` on the class (default-off only on the wrapper), and reaches `SingleWindowAttention` via a new `pad_to_window=False` call-time flag rather than a `window_slots` identity map or a second class.
plan-2026-08-25T053412-0f1fa04f/D-011 | 2026-08-25 | Close the general pad leak by SYNTHESIZING an all-ones key mask whenever the layout creates pad slots and routing it through the existing mask pipeline, rather than hand-building a second layout-aware pad mask.
plan-2026-08-25T053412-0f1fa04f/D-012 | 2026-08-25 | Route ModernBERT's local layers to `'window_band'` at `local_attention_window_size // 2` (span-to-half-width unit conversion, not a shrink) and restore `global_attention_interval=3` on base/large now that D-135's release condition (a working 1-D layer) is met.
plan-2026-08-25T053412-0f1fa04f/D-013 | 2026-08-25 | Retire guards that pinned the old broken behaviour as a positive claim (seed from `zlib.crc32` not salted `hash`; invert the pad-leak-as-positive test to `== 0.0`; rewrite the `'window'`/`'window_zigzag'`/`'window_band'` cost claims per mode, since they no longer share a cost model.
plan-2026-08-25T053412-0f1fa04f/D-014 | 2026-08-25 | Extend the degenerate-single-window short-circuit to `_call_zigzag` (moving `_generate_zigzag_indices` to numpy) after finding every VALUE-comparing instrument in the plan was blind to the 33x cost regression this closes.
plan-2026-08-25T053412-0f1fa04f/D-015 | 2026-08-25 | Move the static window-slot layout off the traced `call()` kwarg channel entirely, into `SingleWindowAttention.set_window_slots()` instance state, because Keras traces a numpy call-kwarg into a symbolic constant and broke `predict()`/`fit()` for every functional consumer.
plan-2026-08-25T053412-0f1fa04f/D-016 | 2026-08-25 | Keep `global_attention_interval=3` after the review's cost challenge: n=3 min-max measurement shows the L=2048 "regression" does not survive repeat draws, and fidelity to the published hybrid architecture outranks a ~4% memory delta at L=4096.
plan-2026-08-25T121346-c71fc3ad/D-006 | 2026-08-25 | Normalize through a module-private `_safe_l2_normalize` that reduces in float32 and floors the squared norm at 1e-12, because `keras.ops.normalize`'s epsilon guard underflows under `mixed_float16` and turns a fully-masked row into NaN.
plan-2026-08-25T121346-c71fc3ad/D-007 | 2026-08-25 | Promote MaxSim's masking and both reductions to float32 whenever the score matrix is reduced-precision, because a fully-masked document's sentinel sum overflows binary16 even after the sentinel itself is clamped.
plan-2026-08-25T121346-c71fc3ad/D-008 | 2026-08-25 | Default `attend_to_mask_tokens=True` so augmented `[MASK]` query slots carry `attention_mask == 1`, preserving the reference's participation semantics under a tokenizer that emits a single mask array for both attention and projection use.
plan-2026-08-25T121346-c71fc3ad/D-009 | 2026-08-25 | Widen the punctuation skiplist to include both the bare and space-prefixed `cl100k_base` token id for every `string.punctuation` symbol, because a literal port of the reference's WordPiece-derived rule filters almost nothing under BPE.
plan-2026-08-25T121346-c71fc3ad/D-010 | 2026-08-25 | Derive the `[Q]`/`[D]` marker ids as `n_vocab - 16` / `n_vocab - 15` from the live encoding and validate no collision at construction, rather than hardcoding them, so a `cl100k_base` vocab-size revision cannot silently alias a content token.
plan-2026-08-25T121346-c71fc3ad/D-011 | 2026-08-25 | Hold exactly one `ColBERTProjection` instance on `self` and route both towers through it, pinned by both weight-count and forward-pass object-identity, so the query and document encoders cannot silently drift into two independently trained projection spaces.
plan-2026-08-25T121346-c71fc3ad/D-012 | 2026-08-25 | Resolve every optional `ColBERT.call` input to a concrete tensor before returning an unconditional three-key dict, because an input-dependent output structure makes `.predict()` raise across batches (the same defect BERT's own D-032 records).
plan-2026-08-25T121346-c71fc3ad/D-014 | 2026-08-25 | Replace `keras.backend.standardize_dtype` with `getattr(dtype, "name", None) or str(dtype)` in `_reduction_dtype`, repairing the tree's one live Keras-2-residue contract violation in-scope rather than deferring it to a later step.
plan-2026-08-25T121346-c71fc3ad/D-015 | 2026-08-25 | Hand-write the v2 distillation KL kernel (`sum_k exp(t_k)*(t_k - s_k)`) rather than reaching for `keras.losses.KLDivergence`, whose probability-domain epsilon clipping makes log-probability inputs collapse to an identically-zero loss and gradient.
plan-2026-08-25T121346-c71fc3ad/D-016 | 2026-08-25 | Return per-row sums from `call` and let the parent Loss's default `sum_over_batch_size` reduction divide by the batch size, reproducing PyTorch's `batchmean` exactly without a custom `__call__` override or an `nway`-fold under-scaling.
plan-2026-08-25T121346-c71fc3ad/D-018 | 2026-08-25 | Re-L2-normalize `ResidualCompressionCodec.decode`'s output as its last operation, anchored so it cannot be swept as dead work, because an un-normalized reconstruction rescales every stored vector and silently corrupts every MaxSim score in the index.
plan-2026-08-25T121346-c71fc3ad/D-025 | 2026-08-25 | Enforce the (query,document)-group row-ordering invariant entirely in the synthetic data producer (group-contiguous rows, `batch_size * nway`, `drop_remainder=True`, shuffle only at group level), because nothing in the flattened tensors themselves records which rows belong together.
plan-2026-08-25T121346-c71fc3ad/D-026 | 2026-08-25 | Attach the loss to `ColBERT`'s `"score"` output key via `model.compile(loss={"score": loss})` and mirror it on the label side, so stock `fit()` supervises only the scalar score and never the two embedding outputs.
plan-2026-08-25T121346-c71fc3ad/D-027 | 2026-08-25 | Keep `SMOKE_PRESET`'s one observably-inert key (`colbert_variant="tiny"`, already the class default) and pin it by name in an explicit vacuity-exemption set asserted by set equality, so a future default change that makes it live turns the guard red instead of being silently absorbed.
plan-2026-08-25T121346-c71fc3ad/D-029 | 2026-08-25 | Split `_encode`'s single collapsed mask into a padding `attention_mask` (backbone-only) and a `participation_mask` (padding + skiplist, projection-only), because the collapsed form fed the punctuation skiplist to the BERT backbone's own attention, silently perturbing every kept position's embedding.
plan-2026-08-25T165753-704a9bcb/D-002 | 2026-08-25 | Delete the model-side `ColBERT.mask_punctuation` field entirely (4 source sites) and replace the presence-only test assertion with a guard that passing `mask_punctuation=` raises `ValueError`, because no trainer ever read it and no checkpoint exists to break.
plan-2026-08-25T165753-704a9bcb/D-009 | 2026-08-25 | Guard the codec's residual stage with a reconstruction-error gain ratio (`error(centroid-only)/error(real) >= 1.3/2.0`), not a MaxSim-drift ordering, after measuring the reviewer's recommended MaxSim ordering false on 6/12 and 8/12 of 12 seeds at this randomly-initialized geometry.
plan-2026-08-25T165753-704a9bcb/D-010 | 2026-08-25 | Anchor `utils/tokenizer.py`'s existing do-not-move-pad-onto-`<|endoftext|>` comment rather than changing any special-token id value, because `cls`/`sep`/`mask` already collide with real `cl100k_base` special tokens and an id change is a behavioural change to a shared util out of this review pass's scope.
plan-2026-08-25T195813-d5a035ab/D-012 | 2026-08-26 | Decide `supports_masking` from the RESOLVED normalization axis via the shared `norms/_masking.py` rule (spelling in `__init__`, exact against the rank in `build()`), never unconditionally `True` and never a spelling-only `axis == -1` test, because `axis=1` leaks 0.914-23.068 across tokens while `axis=2` on a rank-3 input is the feature axis and leaks 0.0.
plan-2026-08-25T195813-d5a035ab/D-004 | 2026-08-25 | Fix RMSNorm/ZeroCenteredRMSNorm's non-trailing-axis scale broadcast at CALL time via a private `_scale_broadcast_shape` computed in `build()` (None on the trailing-axis and unsorted-axis cases), leaving `add_weight`'s built shape byte-identical so no `.keras` checkpoint breaks.
plan-2026-08-25T195813-d5a035ab/D-005 | 2026-08-25 | Retarget the `scale_factors`/`band_param` statistics casts to `stat_dtype` instead of deleting them as B20 claimed was redundant, because a `keras.layers.Dense` returns its own compute dtype, not its input's, making the cast load-bearing against a `mixed_float16` crash.
plan-2026-08-25T195813-d5a035ab/D-014 | 2026-08-26 | Keep `DynamicTanh`'s `alpha_init_value > 0` tightening even though it makes one degenerate checkpoint (negative serialized init value) permanently unloadable, since the repo-wide grep shows no caller can produce one and loosening it would reopen the factory/class validator disagreement B7 reported.
plan-2026-08-26T061816-c515641a/D-004 | 2026-08-26 | `_reshape_for_clustering` returns `(flat, leading)` with `leading` a dynamic tensor read off the concrete input, and `_reshape_output` concatenates it with a provably-static tail, fixing the crash when a layer built via `Input(shape=(None, C))` is later called on a concrete batch.
plan-2026-08-26T061816-c515641a/D-006 | 2026-08-26 | Mask only the data-driven EMA term by cluster mass (`alive = cast(cluster_mass > _MIN_CLUSTER_MASS, dtype)`), asserting distinctiveness by pairwise separation from live centroids rather than by norm, since a dead centroid merges with live ones instead of sitting at the origin. (Threshold later superseded by D-017.)
plan-2026-08-26T061816-c515641a/D-007 | 2026-08-26 | Reject `0 in self.cluster_axis` inside `_setup_cluster_axes`, positioned after negative-to-positive normalization and before the generic range check, naming the batch axis and the cross-sample-leakage consequence.
plan-2026-08-26T061816-c515641a/D-008 | 2026-08-26 | Reject bool `n_clusters`/`units` inline in each layer's own validation (matching GMM's existing idiom), and add a `len(input_shape) < 2` rank guard as the first statement of GMM's and KMeans's `build()`, reusing RBF's existing message text.
plan-2026-08-26T061816-c515641a/D-009 | 2026-08-26 | Emit one `logger.warning` naming the dropped and accepted kwarg sets from `create_mixture_layer` and still return the layer (H-2: warn, never raise); downgrade the per-parameter INFO dump to DEBUG and keep exactly one INFO line.
plan-2026-08-26T061816-c515641a/D-010 | 2026-08-26 | `get_mixture_info()` returns `copy.deepcopy(MIXTURE_REGISTRY)`, with class-identity survival through the deepcopy measured (not assumed) before shipping.
plan-2026-08-26T061816-c515641a/D-011 | 2026-08-26 | Add a pure module-level `_check_registry_literal_consistency` that raises `RuntimeError` naming the symmetric difference between `MixtureType`'s Literal args and `MIXTURE_REGISTRY` keys, called once at import time.
plan-2026-08-26T061816-c515641a/D-012 | 2026-08-26 | Give each of the three mixture layer classes its own `VALID_OUTPUT_MODES` frozenset, have `factory.validate_mixture_config` read `mixture_info['class'].VALID_OUTPUT_MODES` and delete the `if mixture_type == 'rbf'` branch; amend (never delete) the prior `plan-2026-07-20T160907-7de371a1/D-003` comment in place to record why per-class sets still honor it.
plan-2026-08-26T061816-c515641a/D-013 | 2026-08-26 | Extract three separate helpers (`BaseMixtureLayer._init_cluster_axis`, module-level `resolve_initializer_arg`, `resolve_prototype_initializer`) rather than collapsing any two, because their orthonormal-detection predicates are different sets and a shared mode flag would be a shallow module hiding a conditional; switch the orthonormal check from a `__class__.__name__` string sniff to `isinstance`.
plan-2026-08-26T061816-c515641a/D-014 | 2026-08-26 | Name RBF's repulsion safe-sqrt epsilon `_REPULSION_SQRT_EPSILON = 1e-7` as its own module constant rather than unifying it with KMeans's `keras.backend.epsilon()` call, because that backend function is a mutable process-wide global and unifying would couple RBF's gradient stability to any future `set_epsilon` call for zero measured benefit.
plan-2026-08-26T061816-c515641a/D-015 | 2026-08-26 | Keep the inert `centroid_regularizer` parameter (required for config round-trips), warn once at `__init__` when set, and document the inertness plus the `mean_regularizer` asymmetry; rewrite the KMeans docstrings from "end-to-end trained centroids" to an EMA codebook with differentiable assignments, correcting the momentum-nesting formula to show repulsion smoothed inside the EMA.
plan-2026-08-26T061816-c515641a/D-017 | 2026-08-26 | Retarget `_MIN_CLUSTER_MASS` from 1e-3 to 1e-6, derived from the measured epsilon-driven retention curve (no knee, a smooth sigmoid) and the measured `N/K` mass floor of real codebooks, after the adversarial review measured 1e-3 as ~4 orders too high and capable of freezing an entire codebook (1024/1024, 8192/8192, 65536/65536 at batch 1); reword the "held still" docstring claim since a centroid that died with accumulated momentum keeps moving.
plan-2026-08-26T061816-c515641a/D-018 | 2026-08-26 | Repair (not just document) the RBF repulsion mis-calibration: drop the `dim_scale` factor and divide by the true `units*(units-1)` off-diagonal pair count, at the user's direction, after measuring that the removed factor bought no separation (new default 0.1 gives min pairwise distance 2.2383 vs 2.0889 at the old effective `0.1*D=12.8` scale, at ~250x less loss magnitude).
plan-2026-08-26T100331-f3744602/D-008 | 2026-08-26 | Derive `CosineGating`'s temperature floor from the dtype the logits are computed in AND bound the variable itself with `ValueRangeConstraint`, replacing a fixed `keras.backend.epsilon()` floor whose inline comment falsely claimed it prevented inf/NaN under fp16.
plan-2026-08-26T100331-f3744602/D-009 | 2026-08-26 | Cast `gate_logits` to float32 at the top of `compute_z_loss` so it always returns float32, fixing an fp16 overflow to `inf` that poisoned the whole model's loss via `add_loss`; leave `compute_auxiliary_loss` un-upcast, a measured refusal (bounded by construction, finite at every measured scale under `mixed_float16`).
plan-2026-08-26T100331-f3744602/D-010 | 2026-08-26 | Implement `_process_hard_routing_sparse` beside a permanently-retained `_process_hard_routing_dense`, dispatching on `top_k >= num_experts`, gated by an 18-cell-plus-starved-expert numerical equivalence oracle at `atol=1e-5, rtol=0`; no wall-clock threshold gate ships since the dense kernel cannot run the shipped `qwen3_next` preset at all (OOM) while sparse completes in 179.5ms.
plan-2026-08-26T100331-f3744602/D-012 | 2026-08-26 | Add `MoEConfig.__post_init__` validating `num_experts>=1`, `top_k<=num_experts` (excluding `softmoe`, which never routes `top_k` to gating), and `jitter_noise>=0`, closing the one place the `num_experts`/`top_k` cross-field invariant can be checked at all; retain the existing `gating.py` guards as defence in depth.
plan-2026-08-26T100331-f3744602/D-016 | 2026-08-26 | Rename `create_ffn_moe`'s misrouted `use_bias` gating key to `gate_use_bias` and make the factory raise on any undeclared keyword (it was also silently dropping `name=`/`dtype=`/`trainable=`), converting a silent wrong answer into a loud one that names the fix.
plan-2026-08-26T100331-f3744602/D-017 | 2026-08-26 | Document and pin (not normalize) `compute_auxiliary_loss`'s `top_k`-proportional balanced-routing floor (`aux_loss_weight * top_k`, independent of `num_experts`/token count), backed by a 17-case test RED-proven against a normalized mutant, rather than rescale the shipped Switch-Transformer-calibrated regularizer for both production consumers on the strength of an undocumented-interaction finding.
plan-2026-08-26T155709-fb07cf4e/D-005 | 2026-08-27 | Cast `compute_auxiliary_loss`'s return to float32 at the boundary, mirroring `compute_z_loss`'s D-009 idiom, fixing every mixed-precision MoE `model.fit()` crash while leaving float32 numerics bitwise unchanged; amends (does not overwrite) D-017's overflow-was-right-dtype-was-wrong verdict.
plan-2026-08-26T155709-fb07cf4e/D-008 | 2026-08-27 | Widen `_validate_positive_int` to accept `(int, np.integer)`, coerce to `int` (else a numpy scalar detonates `json.dumps` at `model.save()`), keep the `bool`/`np.bool_` rejection first, and add a `2**31-1` representability ceiling with its limits stated rather than an arbitrary tighter cap.
plan-2026-08-27T040114-580f8b63/D-008 | 2026-08-27 | Do not flip the capsule coupling softmax axis as a one-liner (it produces an fp16 NaN on the size-1 non-square branch); instead document the deviation and pin it with a non-square axis-pinning guard.
plan-2026-08-27T040114-580f8b63/D-011 | 2026-08-27 | Return PFA's SW-MSA mask as a numpy constant and convert it at the use site in `call()`, since it is fully derived from `(shift_size, window_size, height, width)` and a tracked `add_weight`/`convert_to_tensor` in `build()` both fail under `StatelessScope`/FuncGraph scoping.
plan-2026-08-27T040114-580f8b63/D-012 | 2026-08-27 | Enforce SingleWindowAttention's documented invariant by refusing a second, different slot map at the same traced length, rather than moving `window_slots` onto the traced `call()` signature (which breaks `predict`/`fit`/`tf.function`).
plan-2026-08-27T040114-580f8b63/D-014 | 2026-08-27 | Build Performer's random projection with `dtype=self.compute_dtype` and create its Dropout unconditionally (gated on `dropout_rate` in `call()`), fixing a mixed_float16 crash with no float32 behavior change.
plan-2026-08-27T040114-580f8b63/D-015 | 2026-08-27 | Clamp NonLocalAttention's additive mask into the compute dtype's finite range in `mask_dtype` rather than routing through `common.apply_attention_mask`, preserving the layer's documented additive-mask contract while eliminating the fp16 -inf/NaN.
plan-2026-08-27T040114-580f8b63/D-016 | 2026-08-27 | Create Dropout unconditionally and gate it on the rate in `call()` for linear_attention, hopfield_attention, and shared_weights_cross_attention (Guide v2 Pitfall 1), without inventing an identity-normalization layer for hopfield's optional norms.
plan-2026-08-27T040114-580f8b63/D-018 | 2026-08-27 | Build shared_weights_cross_attention's Dropout explicitly with the attention-score shape, replacing a dead `pass` stub, per Guide v2 Pitfall 6 (build exactly what `call()` runs).
plan-2026-08-27T040114-580f8b63/D-025 | 2026-08-27 | Add the same undeclared-kwarg set-difference check to `validate_attention_config` that `create_attention_layer` already enforces, closing a silent disagreement between the two doors to the attention registry.
plan-2026-08-27T103353-60745fe0/D-011 | 2026-08-27 | Do not add `package=` to any of the 23 bare `register_keras_serializable()` registrations in `layers/activations/` for guide-v2 s14 Pitfall 3: measured 697 bare registrations repo-wide with 0 colliding keys, and a changed key breaks `load_model` for every stored `.keras` archive holding one of these layers (34/34 exact round-trip today); anchored at `factory.py:37`.
plan-2026-08-28T122601-61a91416/D-003 | 2026-08-28 | Delete `PolarWeightNorm.call`'s unreachable-false `if self.activation is not None:` guard and apply the activation unconditionally, since `keras.activations.get(None)` returns `keras.activations.linear` (never `None`), measured behaviour-preserving bit-for-bit at `activation=None` and `activation='relu'` including a `.keras` round-trip.
plan-2026-08-28T122601-61a91416/D-008 | 2026-08-28 | Compute `EnergyLayerNorm`'s normalization statistics in `keras.backend.result_type(dtype, "float32")` and cast back, following `rms_norm.py:362-372`, fixing a silent `mixed_float16` input-gradient NaN at the default `epsilon=1e-5` (finite forward, 8/16 NaN input gradients) that was silent training death under `LossScaleOptimizer`; proven a dtype fix not a math change (float32 forward bitwise unchanged).
plan-2026-08-28T181715-3870472c/D-001 | 2026-08-28 | Branch `PositionalEmbedding` on the raw `pos_initializer` argument (never the resolved object) so a caller-supplied `TruncatedNormal(stddev=...)` instance survives instead of being silently overwritten by `scale`.
plan-2026-08-28T181715-3870472c/D-003 | 2026-08-28 | Raise `ValueError` in `PositionalEmbedding.build()` when the input exceeds `max_seq_len`, but downgrade that raise to a `logger.warning` inside `build_from_config()` (the real deserialization entry point) via a one-shot flag, so an archived over-long config stays loadable.
plan-2026-08-28T181715-3870472c/D-004 | 2026-08-28 | Raise `ValueError` in `PositionEmbeddingSine2D.__init__` on an odd `num_pos_feats` (it could never complete a forward pass) and add a `from_config` that rounds a stored odd value UP and warns, per guide §6.3.
plan-2026-08-28T181715-3870472c/D-006 | 2026-08-28 | Tighten `Sam3DualViTDetNeck`'s own validation to `d_model % 4 != 0` (the real constraint, since it derives `num_pos_feats = d_model // 2`) with a message naming both fields, and add a `from_config` that rounds a non-conforming stored `d_model` UP to the next multiple of 4 and warns.
plan-2026-08-28T181715-3870472c/D-007 | 2026-08-28 | Apply D-006's even-width shape to the four other `PositionEmbeddingSine2D` construction sites (video_jepa encoder, SAM2 neck, SAM2 memory encoder, DETR model) whose stored configs D-004's raise had made unloadable, each gaining a `% 4` construction check and a `from_config` that rounds up and warns.
plan-2026-08-28T181715-3870472c/D-008 | 2026-08-28 | Tighten `Sam3TransformerDecoder`'s hand-rolled `_sine_embed_for_boxes` (same `d_model // 2` halving, not routed through `PositionEmbeddingSine2D`) to `d_model % 4 != 0`, with a `from_config` shim rounding up to `lcm(4, num_heads)` since `num_heads` must also divide `d_model`.
plan-2026-08-28T181715-3870472c/D-009 | 2026-08-28 | Delete the inert `assert_positive` parameter from `ContinuousRoPE`/`ContinuousSinCosEmbed` rather than implement it, since `tf.debugging.assert_*` is silently dropped under `jit_compile=True` and `keras.ops` has no assert op at all; ship a `from_config` shim that pops a legacy `assert_positive` key with a warning.
plan-2026-08-28T181715-3870472c/D-010 | 2026-08-28 | Add an `epsilon` constructor parameter to `HierarchicalCodebookEmbedding`'s internal `LayerNormalization`, defaulted to Keras' own `1e-3` (what the layer already silently used) rather than the sibling classes' `1e-6`, so no existing checkpoint's output moves.
plan-2026-08-29T043546-e97b34d8/D-004 | 2026-08-29 | Apply `clone_initializer` to BOTH kernel and bias initializers on ALL THREE `GatedMLP` convolutions (six call sites), not only the reported `conv_gate`/`conv_up` kernel pair, fixing gating that provably did not exist (`max|delta| == 0.0` at build and after 5 epochs SGD).
plan-2026-08-29T043546-e97b34d8/D-005 | 2026-08-29 | Apply `clone_initializer` to BOTH kernel and bias initializers on ALL THREE `CountingFFN` Dense layers (six call sites), not only the reported `count_transform`/`gate` kernel pair, since a fix scoped to the guaranteed collision leaves a shape that is silently correlated at another.
plan-2026-08-29T043546-e97b34d8/D-006 | 2026-08-29 | Apply `clone_initializer` to `LowRankFFN`'s kernel initializer on all four Dense layers and to the bias initializer on the two that carry one (`v1`, `v2`), fixing a C-12 defect the inherited dossier missed entirely.
plan-2026-08-29T043546-e97b34d8/D-008 | 2026-08-29 | Clone at every construction site in the ten remaining shared-initializer family files found by the property-based sweep (D-007's scope extension), documenting the shared mechanism ONCE at `glu_ffn.py` with the other nine carrying a pointer, and replacing per-file test classes with one parametrized guard module.
plan-2026-08-29T043546-e97b34d8/D-009 | 2026-08-29 | Remove `SwinMLP`'s `fc1`/`fc2` `activity_regularizer=` forwards, keeping only `self.activity_regularizer` (the base-`Layer` site) as the single penalty on the layer's own output, and anchor the code because a future reader tidying the argument list would silently restore the multi-charge.
plan-2026-08-29T043546-e97b34d8/D-010 | 2026-08-29 | Define `VALID_INTERSECTION_REDUCTIONS`/`VALID_DIFFERENCE_REDUCTIONS` as module-scope frozensets in `tversky_projection.py`, have `factory.py` import them, and validate both in `__init__` with `ValueError`, proving the single-sourcing by executing an edit that reddens both the layer and factory guards together.
plan-2026-08-29T112804-aff039c4/D-001 | 2026-08-29 | Apply `clone_initializer` at every `add_weight` site (7 sites in 3 classes) rather than at each hand-off, and deep-copy the two forwarded kwargs dicts, closing a shared-initializer family the property sweep found in 8 pairs, including a caller-aliasing shape no hand-off clone could see.
plan-2026-08-29T112804-aff039c4/D-002 | 2026-08-29 | Raise `ValueError` in `create_logic_layer` on any kwarg key outside `required_params | optional_params`, with the check living in `validate_logic_config` per v2 §9.2 and Pitfall 15, replacing silent kwarg-dropping.
plan-2026-08-29T112804-aff039c4/D-003 | 2026-08-29 | Emit `"load_balance_coefficient": None` unconditionally in `get_config()` for `CircuitDepthLayer`/`LearnableNeuralCircuit`, since the alias always resolves into the canonical `gate_entropy_coefficient` key and emitting the resolved value would resurrect the deprecated name on every subsequent load.
plan-2026-08-29T112804-aff039c4/D-004 | 2026-08-29 | Replace the unreachable `logger.warning` + silent-identity fallback in both operator `call()` bodies with a `ValueError` naming the offending key and valid set, since `__init__` already validates the same condition and a silent fallback is the guide's dead-knob anti-pattern.
plan-2026-08-29T112804-aff039c4/D-005 | 2026-08-29 | Implement the §16.3 forward-finiteness rule once, as an autouse `conftest.py` fixture wrapping `call()` on all four classes, rather than adding `ops.all(ops.isfinite(y))` to 80 individual test bodies that a future 81st test would silently omit.
plan-2026-08-29T112804-aff039c4/D-006 | 2026-08-29 | Capture the float32 precision control once at module import time (while the global policy is still float32) rather than via a per-layer `dtype="float32"` override, after measuring the override silently ran two of four subjects in float16 because child construction did not propagate dtype.
plan-2026-08-29T112804-aff039c4/D-007 | 2026-08-29 | Pass `dtype=self.dtype_policy`, never `dtype=self.dtype`, at all five child-construction sites in `CircuitDepthLayer`/`LearnableNeuralCircuit`, since Keras 3's `layer.dtype` is the VARIABLE dtype and the 41-file house spelling would silently disable mixed precision for the whole subtree.
plan-2026-08-29T141252-168933da/D-001 | 2026-08-29 | Install the legacy `Custom>X` alias inside the shared decorator at class-definition time, in `keras_registration.py`, rather than a one-shot post-import shim, since 393 of 720 sites are never reached by any package `__init__.py` import.
plan-2026-08-29T141252-168933da/D-002 | 2026-08-29 | Raise `AliasCollisionError` on a genuine second claim of a bare `Custom>{name}` alias (treating the same definition re-executed as idempotent), rather than warn-and-skip, since a silent overwrite is indistinguishable from success and the escape hatch is an explicit `legacy_alias=False` on both sides.
plan-2026-08-29T141252-168933da/D-003 | 2026-08-29 | Add `embeddings_experimental` to the strip list and a `check_strip_list_current()` pre-flight assertion that fails the moment a container-shaped `models/` directory is absent from the list, since a stale entry silently degrades the reorg-immune keying rule to the raw-path rule for everything beneath it.
plan-2026-08-29T141252-168933da/D-004 | 2026-08-29 | Teach the rewriter's scanner to also recognise `register_dl_technique` (not only `register_keras_serializable`), restoring its whole-tree pre-flight assertions to the full 744/493 site count, rather than relaxing the assertions to the selected subset a per-package rewrite pass had shrunk them to.
plan-2026-08-29T141252-168933da/D-005 | 2026-08-29 | Re-key the four `yolov12_losses` sites onto their full module path and carry the old key via a new `legacy_packages=("yolov12_losses",)` generalisation of the existing alias mechanism, since their archives store `yolov12_losses>X` (never `Custom>X`) and the default alias would not have covered them.
plan-2026-08-29T190009-36487cca/D-001 | 2026-08-29 | Compress the 16 (not 17) pre-existing `# DECISION` anchors under `layers/time_series/` toward the brief's 1-5 line shape — anchor id, what the code does, what NOT to change it to, the one measured number — deleting none and renaming none; a site whose truth does not survive 5 lines overshoots and is declared rather than trimmed.
plan-2026-08-30T020716-ebbaf641/D-007 | 2026-08-30 | Delete the manual `self.add_loss(self.activity_regularizer(final_forecast))` at the end of `TemporalFusionLayer.call()`, leaving the penalty to Keras 3's own `Layer.__call__`, since both charges were measured equal and `len(layer.losses)` was 2 not 1.
plan-2026-08-30T020716-ebbaf641/D-008 | 2026-08-30 | Same fix, same commit, for `AdaptiveLagAttentionLayer.call()` — one defect with two instances, not two defects, so the grep gate reads 0 only once both sites are deleted together.
plan-2026-08-30T020716-ebbaf641/D-009 | 2026-08-30 | Reshape `ExogenousBlock` theta DIM-major `(-1, dim, basis_channels)` and contract `einsum('btc,bic->bti')` with no transpose, matching `NBeatsNet.call`'s time-major flatten and `TrendBlock`/`SeasonalityBlock`'s post-transpose layout (verified against an independent numpy oracle to ~1 float32 ulp); the channel-major alternative type-checks and passes every shape assertion but silently transposes the residual stream.
plan-2026-08-30T020716-ebbaf641/D-010 | 2026-08-30 | Keep the old single-channel einsum spelling verbatim under `input_dim == 1 and output_dim == 1` and route the general path to the `else`, after a widened probe (9 shapes x 5 trials, both devices) found the two spellings bit-identical on GPU but non-zero on CPU (worst 4.77e-07) at the sole consumer's own shape — the pre-registered fallback, forced by measurement, not a default special case.
plan-2026-08-30T063229-ccd6ad17/D-014 | 2026-08-30 | Index the `where` result `[0]`, and flatten both the items and the mask to the item axis `compute_input_quality` actually scores before gathering, fixing `filter_by_quality_threshold`'s `TypeError` at every input rank.
plan-2026-08-30T063229-ccd6ad17/D-015 | 2026-08-30 | Append the four newly-exposed `create_ntm` parameters (`addressing_mode`, `use_memory_init`, `memory_init_seed`, `epsilon`) at the END of the signature, not grouped with `NTMConfig`'s field order, so no existing positional call silently re-binds.
plan-2026-08-30T063229-ccd6ad17/D-016 | 2026-08-30 | Anchor the `sharpness_weight > 0` + `use_per_dimension_softmax=False` raise at its site as a hard construction-time failure — do not soften to a warning or make the loss reachable on the global path.
plan-2026-08-30T063229-ccd6ad17/D-017 | 2026-08-30 | Replace the einsum subscript cap with a named module-level constant `GRID_EINSUM_SUBSCRIPTS = 'ijklmnopqrstuvwxy'` (17 letters, `'i'`..`'y'`), since the carried "cap should be 18" repair was measured wrong and raises `InvalidArgumentError`.
plan-2026-08-30T063229-ccd6ad17/D-019 | 2026-08-30 | Resolve each shared-instance constructor default (grid_initializer, grid_regularizer, kernel_regularizer) via a sentinel inside `__init__` before the `keras.*.get(...)` normalisation, asymmetric by type (`None`-sentinel for the non-Optional param, a per-file module-level constant for the two Optional ones), so every caller who omits the argument gets a fresh object.
plan-2026-08-30T063229-ccd6ad17/D-023 | 2026-08-30 | Resolve a bare-name instance-default to its module-level binding and judge THAT (not just the default expression node), allowing only a bare `object()` as a safe sentinel, after the guard was proven blind to the exact house pattern its own fix introduced.
plan-2026-08-30T063229-ccd6ad17/D-024 | 2026-08-30 | Keep all 6 `.keras` round-trip assertions at `atol=0.0, rtol=0.0` rather than the v2 guide's example `atol=1e-6`, since a round trip restores weights by copy (no float reassociation) and every site measures exactly 0.0.
plan-2026-08-30T120217-7f6cedd1/D-002 | 2026-08-30 | Remove `epsilon` from `BaseMemory.__init__`/`NTMMemory.__init__`/`BaseMemory.get_config` and add a `from_config` classmethod on `BaseMemory` that pops only the named legacy key with a `logger.warning` before `cls(**config)`, refusing to generalize to a blanket unknown-key filter.
plan-2026-08-30T152113-67a8cc1e/D-001 | 2026-08-30 | Keep `deep_kernel_pca.py`'s `_fit_flag` exactly as-is (never read, never deleted) — deleting it drops the stored-weight count from 6 to 5, so `load_own_variables` mis-detects a surplus in every existing `.keras` checkpoint and raises `IndexError` on load.
plan-2026-08-30T175846-3e8a6ff3/D-002 | 2026-08-30 | Fix each of the 4 never-narrow dtype sites inline with a `dtype=`/`ops.cast` argument sourced from the in-scope tensor, rather than a shared helper — four sites do not earn an abstraction (YAGNI), and a fifth site is caught by the package-wide rule scan guard, not by inheritance.
plan-2026-08-30T175846-3e8a6ff3/D-004 | 2026-08-30 | Build `NormalizingFlowLayer` explicitly at the top of `forward`/`inverse`/`sample` when unbuilt, preserving today's `build()`-produced weight paths exactly, since those three entry points bypass `Layer.__call__` and previously left weights outside the layer's name scope (a bare `dense_1/kernel` instead of `flow/affine_coupling_i/...`).
plan-2026-08-30T191258-29fae917/D-002 | 2026-08-30 | Return the per-sample `(batch,)` vector `(forward + backward) / 2.0` from `call()` and let the parent `Loss` reduce, matching `CLIPContrastiveLoss` and both ColBERT losses, since a scalar return is measured to broadcast `sample_weight` across the whole batch aggregate rather than bypass it.
plan-2026-08-30T191258-29fae917/D-004 | 2026-08-30 | Raise `ValueError` when the batch axis is statically 1 (a 1x1 logit matrix softmaxes to exactly 1.0, giving an exact-zero-gradient no-op step), and document but do not guard the dynamic-batch case, directing callers to `drop_remainder=True`.
plan-2026-08-30T203107-30455f66/D-001 | 2026-08-30 | Make the `train_clip.py` reduction call polymorphic (`keras.ops.mean` over whatever the loss returns) instead of adding a per-class `reduced_loss` method, since `--loss siglip` was dead on arrival calling a method only `CLIPContrastiveLoss` defines, and a shared mixin was rejected as an unearned abstraction.
plan-2026-08-31T045723-c0d5ffa9/D-001 | 2026-08-31 | Build the `stable_max` branch's LM sub-loss with `reduction="none"`, matching the `sparse_categorical_crossentropy` branch, since `call()` divides `sum(lm_losses)/sum(valid_counts)` and without it `Loss.__call__` pre-averages the scalar so that line divides the LM term a second time (measured 24x-4096x under-weighting; both shipped HRM/TRM trainers default to this branch).
plan-2026-08-31T045723-c0d5ffa9/D-002 | 2026-08-31 | Keep `StableMaxCrossEntropy.call()` returning `(batch, seq_len)` rather than reducing it to `(batch,)`, after measuring the recommended reduction breaks value-preservation by 42.86% under masking, duplicates the stock Keras token-shaped convention, and would require `HRMLoss` to multiply the sequence length back in.
plan-2026-08-31T045723-c0d5ffa9/D-003 | 2026-08-31 | Clamp ignore_index-masked labels to class 0 before `HRMLoss`'s `sparse_categorical_crossentropy` sub-loss sees them, then zero those positions in `lm_losses` afterward, since `ignore_class` cannot express a negative sentinel and passing `-100` through raw crashes at the class's own default.
plan-2026-08-31T095434-b4829a10/D-002 | 2026-08-31 | Create the sub-layer unconditionally in `__init__` with an explicit `name="top_down_upsample"`, materialize it unconditionally in `build()` on the fused `(batch, h, w, d_model)` shape following the in-file `position_encoding` precedent, and use it in `call()` with no construction.
plan-2026-08-31T121200-753e0d87/D-005 | 2026-08-31 | Every `LayerScale` call site replacing a hand-rolled gamma passes `multiplier_type="CHANNEL"`, `initializer=keras.initializers.Constant(<layer_scale_init>)` and `constraint=None` explicitly, since the layer's defaults (`initializer="ones"`, `constraint="non_neg"`) fail silently and numerically on a residual-branch scale.
plan-2026-08-31T134711-6271592d/D-007 | 2026-08-31 | Convert all seven additive mask-bias sites (softmax, argmax, and ops.max/ops.min reductions) to the same `ops.where(keep_bool, x, cast(mask_sentinel(compute_dtype)))` idiom, calling `mask_sentinel` directly at each site and adopting `apply_attention_mask` at none of them, since the defect is the multiplication itself and every site is already finite-but-degenerate on an all-masked row under the where form.
plan-2026-08-31T134711-6271592d/D-009 | 2026-08-31 | Route four confirmed fp16-inert floors (TextSimilarityHead cosine, both kernel-PCA whitening divides, EMA weight floor) through `stability_floor(compute_dtype, <original literal>)`, and at ema_layer.py delete the `ops.cast` entirely since the cast itself was the mechanism that zeroed the epsilon.
plan-2026-08-31T134711-6271592d/D-010 | 2026-08-31 | Add two minimal enabling dtype fixes (`ops.eye(..., dtype=kernel_matrix.dtype)`, `ops.cast(ops.sqrt(...), cos_features.dtype)`) since both kernel-PCA layers were dead on forward pass under mixed_float16 and float64, raising before the floor fix this step exists to deliver could ever be reached.
plan-2026-08-31T134711-6271592d/D-014 | 2026-08-31 | Add a third public function `accumulation_dtype(dtype)` to dtype_policy.py and promote every ADDITIVE-epsilon site (xLSTM's four log/divide floors, DeepKernelPCA's cosine/sigmoid kernel branch) to float32 before flooring, since `stability_floor`'s float16 return of 6.10e-05 was measured injecting up to +6.21 nats and a 610% bias into regimes that were exact under the original bare 1e-8/1e-10 literal.
plan-2026-08-31T134711-6271592d/D-016 | 2026-09-01 | Route NAMCell's halt-divisor epsilon through `accumulation_dtype` + `stability_floor` instead of the hand-rolled `np.finfo(compute_dtype).tiny`, which raises `ValueError` under `mixed_bfloat16` (numpy 2.0.2 cannot resolve bfloat16 to an inexact type) — the exact fact D-006 recorded and routed around inside dtype_policy.py.
plan-2026-08-31T175140-a4e0c303/D-010 | 2026-09-01 | Pin the DEFINITION of `canonical_binary_input_shape` tree-wide (exactly 1 `def`, 0 `def _canonical_input_shape`) but pin the `is_list_of_shapes = (` detection SUBSTRING package-scoped (exactly once in `utils/tensors.py`, zero times under `layers/logic/`), because the obvious tree-wide substring widening asserts a merge `layers/attention/` has explicitly refused in writing at `multi_head_cross_attention.py:505-515` (three inline copies disagree about accepting a `tuple` and about numpy shape elements) — a guard demanding a behaviour change is not a guard.
plan-2026-08-31T175140-a4e0c303/D-011 | 2026-09-01 | `import_pyplot` uses SETDEFAULT semantics, never an override: a non-empty `MPLBACKEND` is respected and only an unset one selects `Agg`. Do NOT restore the unconditional `matplotlib.use("Agg")` — it discarded a caller's explicit backend to fix a headless crash that DOES NOT REPRODUCE (measured on matplotlib 3.10.0: `MPLBACKEND` unset + `DISPLAY` unset -> `agg`; bogus `DISPLAY=:99` -> `agg` with `savefig` OK). Do NOT key the detection off `matplotlib.get_backend()` instead: it cannot distinguish a prior `use()` from the default resolution, so the env var is the only honest "explicitly requested" signal.
plan-2026-08-31T175140-a4e0c303/D-014 | 2026-09-01 | Keep the explicit `n >= 1` clause in `utils/tensors.py::is_power_of_two`; do NOT collapse it to the bare `n & (n - 1) == 0` bit trick, which accepts `0` (`0 & -1 == 0`) and would admit a zero-width dimension into both binary-tree consumers (`layers/orthogonal_butterfly.py`, `layers/norms/polar_weight_norm.py`). Neither consumer suite can see the difference — `0` is the sole discriminating input and both reject it earlier for their own reasons — so the guard is `tests/test_utils/test_tensors.py::TestIsPowerOfTwo` alone.
plan-2026-08-31T175140-a4e0c303/D-015 | 2026-09-01 | `layers/transformers/sd3_adaln.py::modulate` is PUBLIC (renamed from `_modulate`) because `models/vision_language/sd3_mmdit/blocks.py` imports it across a package boundary and a leading underscore on such a name is a privacy marker that lies. Do NOT re-privatise it, do NOT leave a `_modulate` compatibility alias (the alias restores the lie), and do NOT unify it with `AdaLNZeroConditionalBlock._modulate` — this one expands `(B, dim)` shift/scale to `(B, 1, dim)` itself and the other does not.
plan-2026-08-31T175140-a4e0c303/D-016 | 2026-09-01 | `AdaLNZeroConditionalBlock._modulate` stays PRIVATE and stays put: the CALLER owns the broadcast (both call sites pass already-aligned `(B, T, D)` chunks) and nothing outside its module calls it, so its underscore is honest. Visibility tracks reach, not similarity. Merging it with `sd3_adaln.modulate` would silently move a `(B, dim)` operand from the sample axis to the sequence axis.
plan-2026-08-31T175140-a4e0c303/D-017 | 2026-09-01 | `models/vision_language/sd3_mmdit/blocks.py` imports `modulate` from `sd3_adaln` and must never re-define it locally; the anchor sits on the import because the ABSENCE of a local definition is the decision. A re-added copy would drift from the owner's broadcast contract while every test in this package kept passing, which is why the detecting guard lives in the layers suite.
plan-2026-08-31T175140-a4e0c303/D-018 | 2026-09-01 | `models/vision_language/sd3_mmdit/config.py` imports `validate_vae_groupnorm` from its ideogram4 owner (public since fix 3.2, same privacy-lie reason as D-015) and must never re-declare it; a shadowing local copy stays green against every test that imports the name from ideogram4 rather than from this module, so the sd3-side guard imports it from THIS module and one injection must redden BOTH suites.
plan-2026-09-01T055648-e6d380a5/D-001 | 2026-09-01 | Extend `standard_blocks.ConvBlock` with `groups` / `use_bias` / activation-off (routed through `resolve_activation_layer`) rather than abandon the swap, migrate all yolo12 call sites onto it, then delete `yolo12_blocks.ConvBlock` — the user-selected approach after F-1 established the two classes shared only a name.
plan-2026-09-01T055648-e6d380a5/D-003 | 2026-09-01 | Give the relocated `AreaAttention` full `attention/GUIDE.md` compliance (`ProbabilityOutput`, `common.compute_attention_scale` computed once in `__init__`, `apply_attention_mask` with a derived `rescue_axis`, `qk_norm_type`) rather than a bare port, pre-registering the scale-substitution rule that a non-bit-identical spelling is accepted only if the measured output delta stays within the step-3 equivalence tolerance.
plan-2026-09-01T055648-e6d380a5/D-005 | 2026-09-01 | Give the pinned D-067 BatchNorm pair (`epsilon=1e-3, momentum=0.97`) exactly one home — `YOLO12_NORM_KWARGS` in `yolo12_blocks.py`, carrying the D-067 rationale verbatim — and thread it as data (`normalization_kwargs`) into `AreaAttention`/`AreaAttentionBlock` rather than hardcoding it a second time or having `layers/attention/` import `layers/yolo12_blocks.py`.
plan-2026-09-01T110541-dcc1574a/D-001 | 2026-09-01 | Rename the narrower consumer in each colliding pair (PWFNetDownsample, PWFNetUpsample, TabMMLPBlock) so all 8 `legacy_alias=False` sites drop the flag; the un-renamed halves (ffn.MLPBlock, ideogram4's Downsample/Upsample) carry anchors stating why they keep the bare name.
plan-2026-09-01T110541-dcc1574a/D-005 | 2026-09-01 | Build tests/test_the_legacy_alias_namespace_has_no_collisions.py as an executable AST+runtime census guard over all `Custom>` registration roots, proven RED at HEAD before the fix, three arms xfail(strict) until the renames land.
plan-2026-09-01T163450-1f49c11f/D-002 | 2026-09-01 | Introduce one registered `AccUNetFunctional` keras.Model subclass carrying the same `compile`/`compile_from_config` overrides as `AccUNet`, and have all three `create_acc_unet*` factories build it instead of a bare `keras.Model`, sharing the XLA-forcing rule via one module-level `_force_no_xla(kwargs)` helper.
plan-2026-09-01T175024-5a32e889/D-001 | 2026-09-01 | Ship ONE core function `soft_value_range` in `src/dl_techniques/layers/activations/soft_value_range.py` alongside the `SoftValueRange` Layer (the `mish.py` function+Layer-in-one-file precedent), with `SoftValueRangeConstraint` as a thin wrapper in `src/dl_techniques/constraints/`; assert feasibility unconditionally and strict interiority / nonzero gradient only inside a regime whose probe distance is derived from `beta` and the dtype's own spacing, and say in the docstrings which regime each guarantee describes.
plan-2026-09-01T175024-5a32e889/D-002 | 2026-09-01 | Keep the `enforce_hard_bounds` flag, but re-found it on the only effect it can have — removing the small lower-bound undershoot the two-branch composition can produce when `hi - lo` is comparable to `1/beta` — and gate it on measurement rather than the brief's false "reintroduces zero gradients" framing (Keras applies constraints via `variable.assign` after the optimizer step, outside any tape, so a constraint never sees a gradient).
plan-2026-09-01T175024-5a32e889/D-003 | 2026-09-01 | Follow the step brief and import only `soft_value_range` into the constraint (no cross-package reach into `_validated_bounds`), keeping a deliberate second copy of the two validation checks in `SoftValueRangeConstraint.__init__`, but make their lockstep mechanical via a shared `INVALID_PARAMS` table in the test file that feeds both entry points.
plan-2026-09-01T201957-15d7a40e/D-002 | 2026-09-01 | Adopt the new SoftOrthonormalConstraintRegularizer default at diff_ffn.py:335 by passing all four coefficients explicitly, pinning l2_coefficient=0.0, guarded by a get_config() test.
plan-2026-09-01T225724-e79ad4bd/D-002 | 2026-09-02 | Keep the single flattened conv matricization and set `rf = 1.0` for CONV2D/3D, anchoring at the reshape that this is a DELIBERATE DIVERGENCE from WeightWatcher's per-slice decomposition (per-slice was rejected: it would move alpha/entropy/gini/learning_phase for every conv layer of every model ever analyzed).
plan-2026-09-01T225724-e79ad4bd/D-003 | 2026-09-02 | Arbitrate the rank tolerance against WeightWatcher's own `RMT_Util.matrix_rank` BEFORE shipping, and ship as a recorded divergence rather than a parity claim if WW disagrees; the fix captures the source dtype's eps before the float64 promotion (resolved by D-018).
plan-2026-09-01T225724-e79ad4bd/D-008 | 2026-09-02 | Filter spectral aggregates by the EXISTING `status` column (`StatusCode.SUCCESS`), never by a sentinel VALUE, and report `successful_layers_analyzed`/`failed_layers` beside the means while `total_layers_analyzed` keeps its published meaning.
plan-2026-09-01T225724-e79ad4bd/D-009 | 2026-09-02 | Floor the concentration denominator (`lambda_max / max(sum_others, SPECTRAL_EPSILON)`) instead of short-circuiting to `0.0`; the stability concern is already handled by the `np.log1p` on `concentration_score`.
plan-2026-09-01T225724-e79ad4bd/D-010 | 2026-09-02 | Return the documented `-1.0` "not computed" sentinel from every could-not-compute exit of the power-law goodness-of-fit, and add a keyword-only `d_observed` so the caller passes the KS distance of the fit it actually reports instead of triggering an internal refit.
plan-2026-09-01T225724-e79ad4bd/D-011 | 2026-09-02 | Gini bias fix is `+ 1/n`; the `n/(n-1)` rescaling that the plan's own step text prescribed is REFUTED and is NOT implemented.
plan-2026-09-01T225724-e79ad4bd/D-012 | 2026-09-02 | Compute `log_alpha_norm` in log space (`logsumexp(alpha*log(max(evals, eps)))/log(10)`), publish an `alpha_unreliable` flag keyed off `SPECTRAL_ALPHA_SANITY_MAX = 8.0`, and replace the blanket `simplefilter("ignore")` with two message-scoped `filterwarnings` calls.
plan-2026-09-01T225724-e79ad4bd/D-013 | 2026-09-02 | Leave `compute_mp_softrank` byte-for-byte alone and change only the WIRING to WeightWatcher's theoretical-edge form `calc_mp_soft_rank(evals, N, M)` built on a shared `calc_mp_edges(sigma_sq, Q)` helper factored out of `detect_correlation_trap`.
plan-2026-09-01T225724-e79ad4bd/D-014 | 2026-09-02 | Make the Brier decomposition a genuine binary Murphy decomposition of the TOP-1 Brier score on a shared `_get_binary_bin_info` binner, returning `brier_score` (which the three terms reproduce EXACTLY) and `binning_residual`; fix it rather than delete it.
plan-2026-09-01T225724-e79ad4bd/D-015 | 2026-09-02 | Publish true Kull classwise-ECE under the existing `per_class_ece` key and keep the old quantity under the honest new key `per_class_conditional_top1_ece` — a REDEFINITION, so pre-plan stored results compare against a different quantity.
plan-2026-09-01T225724-e79ad4bd/D-016 | 2026-09-02 | Bin the ESD in `log10` space over `[log10(max(min, eps)), log10(max)]`, flooring each eigenvalue at `SPECTRAL_EPSILON`, matching the log-log ESD plots the rest of the module draws.
plan-2026-09-01T225724-e79ad4bd/D-017 | 2026-09-02 | Estimate the bulk variance with spikes excluded (`estimate_bulk_variance`, iterative re-mean below the MP edge), average correlation-trap detection over `config.spectral_n_randomizations` draws with a MAJORITY vote, and share the severity ladder via `label_trap_severity`; a DELIBERATE DIVERGENCE from `CORRELATION_TRAPS.md`, not WW parity.
plan-2026-09-01T225724-e79ad4bd/D-019 | 2026-09-02 | `compute_eigenvalues` returns `spectrum_truncated`, decided from the RESULT (`len(sv) < min(W.shape)`) not from the branch condition; on that path `sv_min`/`rank_loss`/`weak_rank_loss`/`entropy`/`matrix_rank` are `nan` rather than plausible wrong numbers.
plan-2026-09-01T225724-e79ad4bd/D-020 | 2026-09-02 | `_analyze_single_model` returns `(details, esds, rand_esds, recommendations, summary)` and `_analyze_layers` fills `esds`/`rand_esds` as documented output parameters; the four `self._*` attributes and their unfalsifiable `hasattr` guards are deleted.
plan-2026-09-01T225724-e79ad4bd/D-021 | 2026-09-02 | Record the invocation index inside `recording_call` (which runs at true forward time) as `capture_index` on every per-layer entry, and have `_get_ordered_layer_analysis` SORT on it instead of echoing dict order; `recursively_get_layers` is NOT touched.
plan-2026-09-01T225724-e79ad4bd/D-022 | 2026-09-02 | Wire `memory_limit_mb` as a real cap on the information-flow CAPTURE BATCH, sized by a throwaway B=1 pass through the already-wrapped layers, default changed `None` -> `2048`; `None` still means explicitly unbounded.
plan-2026-09-01T225724-e79ad4bd/D-023 | 2026-09-02 | Discover sublayers by iterating `vars(layer)` (the instance `__dict__`) in attribute-assignment order rather than `dir()`, and delete the blanket `except Exception` along with the `getattr` it guarded.
plan-2026-09-01T225724-e79ad4bd/D-024 | 2026-09-02 | The detX tail index is `np.flatnonzero(tail_log_det[1:] < 0.0)[-1] + 1` (LAST negative index). REFUTATION of the plan's own step text and findings file, which both prescribed `np.argmax(rev < 0.0)` — the WRONG DIRECTION, which would have shipped a wrong projection rank into a production optimizer.
plan-2026-09-01T225724-e79ad4bd/D-026 | 2026-09-02 | Use `scipy.sparse.linalg.svds(W, k=k)` when `k < min(shape)`, sorting its ascending output descending; keep the full `np.linalg.svd` for a full-rank request and as an explicit fallback when ARPACK does not converge.
plan-2026-09-01T225724-e79ad4bd/D-027 | 2026-09-02 | The spectral truncation and minimum-tail bounds become keyword-only `None`-defaulted PARAMETERS falling back to the module constants, so `config.spectral_max_evals`/`spectral_min_evals` actually control what they are documented to control; do not re-inline the constants.
plan-2026-09-01T225724-e79ad4bd/D-028 | 2026-09-02 | Delete the eight dead `AnalysisConfig` fields, WIRE `json_include_raw_esds` in `save_results`, and pin config-vs-README agreement in BOTH directions with an executable guard so the table cannot drift again.
plan-2026-09-01T225724-e79ad4bd/D-029 | 2026-09-02 | Declare `_original_rcParams` as a real dataclass field, add `restore_plotting_style()` so the captured state has a reader, and serialize the config from `dataclasses.fields()` filtered on `not name.startswith('_')` instead of `__dict__`-minus-a-magic-string.
plan-2026-09-01T225724-e79ad4bd/D-030 | 2026-09-02 | Publish `relative_overfitting_index` and `stability_cv` ALONGSIDE the raw untouched training keys, label the Pareto x axis with the units caveat, and document `epochs_to_convergence`'s own-peak weakness in the docstring rather than changing it.
plan-2026-09-01T225724-e79ad4bd/D-031 | 2026-09-02 | Add `random_state` to `AnalysisConfig` plus one `utils.make_rng` helper, and thread a `numpy.random.Generator` to all five stochastic sites as an optional keyword-only `rng` parameter.
plan-2026-09-01T225724-e79ad4bd/D-032 | 2026-09-02 | Add `output_activation` (`softmax`/`sigmoid`/`logits`) to `AnalysisConfig`, obeyed when set; when unset `_infer_output_activation` returns one of those three and ONLY `logits` is softmaxed, with the inference logged at INFO.
plan-2026-09-01T225724-e79ad4bd/D-033 | 2026-09-02 | Request equal aspect on the PCA panel only when the two component spans are COMMENSURATE (`max_span/min_span <= 1e3`), otherwise leave the default aspect and log at DEBUG; the README gains an honest note that a figure failure is caught by `_save_figure` and only visible in the log.
plan-2026-09-01T225724-e79ad4bd/D-035 | 2026-09-02 | `calculate_glorot_normalization_factor` takes the ORDERED matricized shape plus `rf` and computes `fan_in = rows`, `fan_out = cols * rf` (Keras' own convolutional fan convention); `(N, M)` is no longer accepted, because the old form was wrong for every kernel with `out_c > kh*kw*in_c`.
plan-2026-09-01T225724-e79ad4bd/D-036 | 2026-09-02 | Resolve accuracy from `model.get_metrics_result()` merged into the metric dict, aliased by metric CLASS first (walking the Keras 3 `CompileMetrics` container) and by name second; when no accuracy metric exists report `None` (`ACCURACY_UNAVAILABLE`), never `0.0`.
plan-2026-09-01T225724-e79ad4bd/D-037 | 2026-09-02 | Compute ONE information-flow batch size for the whole run — the MINIMUM of each model's affordable batch, in a `_shared_batch_size` pre-pass — and record it as `AnalysisResults.information_flow_batch_size`; this is a correctness bug, not a tuning knob.
plan-2026-09-01T225724-e79ad4bd/D-038 | 2026-09-02 | Add a second sublayer-discovery pass over class-level `property` descriptors, skipping any property NAME declared by a KERAS-owned base derived from `__mro__` at call time rather than from a hand-written list.
plan-2026-09-01T225724-e79ad4bd/D-039 | 2026-09-02 | Give `ModelAnalyzer` `__enter__`/`__exit__` with `__exit__` calling `config.restore_plotting_style()`, and document that this context manager is the ONLY shipped caller — a plain `ModelAnalyzer(...)` still leaves the global plotting style applied.
plan-2026-09-01T225724-e79ad4bd/D-040 | 2026-09-02 | Stamp `schema_version` (`RESULTS_SCHEMA_VERSION = 2`) and `analyzer_version` into every artifact `save_results` writes, and document the calibration redefinitions in the README table, because several published metric VALUES moved in this plan.
plan-2026-09-01T225724-e79ad4bd/D-042 | 2026-09-02 | Clamp gini at `max(0.0, float(gini))` so the documented range is literally true, and soften the uniform-spectrum claim to "0.0 up to float round-off (measured |g| <= 1.3e-15)"; D-011's `+1/n` bias fix is untouched.
plan-2026-09-02T041737-e85f2027/D-001 | 2026-09-02 | Evaluate the power-law CDF in the log domain as `1 - exp((1 - alpha) * (log_data[i:] - log_data[i]))` at BOTH `pow` sites in the xmin sweep, reusing the `log_data` already computed above the loop, and hoist the ECDF `np.arange` ramp to one array sliced per candidate.
plan-2026-09-02T041737-e85f2027/D-003 | 2026-09-02 | Pin ARPACK's start vector in `get_top_eigenvectors`' `direct` path to the constant `np.full(min_dim, 1/sqrt(min_dim))`, because a defaulted `v0=None` drew from numpy's unseeded global legacy RNG and made four published columns nondeterministic within a single process.
plan-2026-09-02T041737-e85f2027/D-004 | 2026-09-02 | Vectorise critical-weight enumeration behind a new `summarize_critical_weights` (boolean mask plus `np.count_nonzero`, top-k via `argpartition`), REFUSING the plan's prescription to skip the call — `critical_weight_count` is a published column, not dead output.
plan-2026-09-02T041737-e85f2027/D-005 | 2026-09-02 | Replace the correlation-trap offset with Johnstone (2001) `Delta_TW = c_TW * lambda_plus * M**(-2/3) * Q**(-1/6) * (1 + sqrt(Q))**(-2/3)` and supersede the legacy anchor `plan_2026-06-03_bc986e52/D-003` IN PLACE by appendix (paragraph retained verbatim), because its "default 1.0 = WW-exact" premise was false — the port dropped WeightWatcher's W-scale normalize/un-rescale pair.
plan-2026-09-02T041737-e85f2027/D-006 | 2026-09-02 | Raise `SPECTRAL_TW_SAFETY_FACTOR` 1.0 -> 3.0 and bump `RESULTS_SCHEMA_VERSION` 2 -> 3, now that the corrected offset makes the knob non-inert: false-positive rate on clean Wisharts falls from 0.087-0.122 to 0.000-0.010 while power stays 1.000 at element amplitude >= 20.
plan-2026-09-02T062406-e2aa52ef/D-001 | 2026-09-02 | Compute weight statistics in the tensor's OWN dtype first and use `float64` only to REPAIR the individual leaves that came out non-finite (plus unconditional promotion for non-floating dtypes), so healthy models keep bit-identical statistics and PCA coordinates.
plan-2026-09-02T062406-e2aa52ef/D-002 | 2026-09-02 | Two distinct StatusCode members: `WEIGHT_DEGENERATE` ('degenerate') for a finite, legal tensor whose leaf was recomputed or substituted (itemised in a new `degenerate_fields` list), and `WEIGHT_NON_FINITE` ('non-finite') for genuinely corrupt weights, which are never repaired — a corrupt model must stay distinguishable from a merely degenerate one.
plan-2026-09-02T062406-e2aa52ef/D-003 | 2026-09-02 | Add `LOSS_UNAVAILABLE = None` beside `ACCURACY_UNAVAILABLE` and use it on both `_evaluate_models` failure branches and in `get_summary_statistics`, bumping `RESULTS_SCHEMA_VERSION` 3 -> 4; `loss = 0.0` was a failure sentinel that reads as a PERFECT model.
