plan-2026-09-02T062406-e2aa52ef/D-004 | 2026-09-02 | `find_model_metric`'s default becomes `None` and a `_format_metric` helper renders `None`/non-finite as 'n/a', because the dashboard's numeric `0.0` default was silently converting the analyzer's deliberate `accuracy=None` back into a printed `0.000`.
plan-2026-09-02T062406-e2aa52ef/D-005 | 2026-09-02 | `_evaluate_models`' outer `except Exception` now wraps ONLY the `model.evaluate` call so an analyzer-side bug in the post-processing surfaces instead of being downgraded to a per-model `status: 'error'`; the inner `(ValueError, TypeError, RuntimeError)` tuple is unchanged and gains `exc_info=True`.
plan-2026-09-02T062406-e2aa52ef/D-006 | 2026-09-02 | An `np.isfinite(weights).all()` pre-check before the rank-2 `np.linalg.norm(weights, 2)` call in `_raw_statistics`, so a corrupt model no longer drives a NaN matrix into LAPACK and emits unattributable `DLASCL` noise on raw stderr; the published value is `nan` either way.
plan-2026-09-02T062406-e2aa52ef/D-007 | 2026-09-02 | `_raw_statistics_quietly` captures warnings around that one call and re-emits them at their original location UNLESS the category is `RuntimeWarning` AND the statistics were classified degenerate/non-finite — the one case where `status`/`degenerate_fields` already record the same fact; deliberately not a blanket `simplefilter('ignore')`.
plan-2026-09-02T081011-9b26b501/D-001 | 2026-09-02 | Delete the mutual-information term outright (parameter, method and the `ops.maximum(0.0, mi)` clamp), replace it with a correctly-signed off-by-default `prior_weight * KL(prior || mean_p)` anti-collapse term, move the confidence penalty into the `(batch,)` vector so `sample_weight` weights it per row, swap clip+log for `keras.ops.log_softmax`, and keep `mi_weight` as a keyword that only raises a `ValueError` naming `prior_weight`.
plan-2026-09-02T081011-9b26b501/D-004 | 2026-09-02 | Accept an explicit `dtype` argument, forward it to `super().__init__`, store the caller's argument verbatim as `self._dtype_arg` and emit THAT in `get_config()` — never the resolved `self.dtype`, which would pin the live global policy into every saved config.
plan-2026-09-02T081011-9b26b501/D-005 | 2026-09-02 | Compute the `from_logits=False` clip floor as `max(self.epsilon, np.finfo(standardize_dtype(y_pred.dtype)).tiny)` and renormalize, because `epsilon=1e-8` is below float16's smallest normal so a bare literal clip floors to 0.0 and `log(0)` propagates NaN.
plan-2026-09-02T081011-9b26b501/D-006 | 2026-09-02 | `analyze_loss_components` reports `*_magnitude` and `*_share` = `|component| / sum|components|` instead of `component / total_loss * 100`, which was unbounded because the total is a signed sum (measured ce = -100% with entropy = +200% at a negative total).
plan-2026-09-02T094601-77d4a04e/D-011 | 2026-09-02 | Keep the `maximum(variance, 0)` clamp on the bridge posterior variance and pin it with two MEASURED float32 negatives (`UniformVolatilitySDE(A=5,K=1)` at t=1 -> -7.45e-09; `PeriodicVolatilitySDE(0.95,k=3,eps=1e-3)` at t=0.99875 -> -2.98e-08); at the three SHIPPED defaults no negative exists over 2,010,002 samples, so the clamp is demonstrated off-default and defensive at the defaults.
plan-2026-09-02T094601-77d4a04e/D-012 | 2026-09-02 | Port ONE range mechanism per time sampler exactly as upstream has it — the affine `u*(1-2eps)+eps` map for the uniform sampler, `clip` for the logit-normal one — and add neither the missing clip nor the missing affine map, because two redundant mechanisms make either one deletable with every containment arm still green.
plan-2026-09-02T094601-77d4a04e/D-004 | 2026-09-02 | Build the 12-way adaLN modulation inside `DiTXABlock` from one zero-init `Dense(12*hidden)` plus `ops.split`, reusing only the shared `modulate()` free function from `sd3_adaln.py`; add no new class under `layers/transformers/`.
plan-2026-09-02T094601-77d4a04e/D-005 | 2026-09-02 | Make `direction` a per-sample input tensor: both conditioning embedders are always built and run, and `ops.where` selects between their outputs, the `t_cond` endpoints, and `forward_cond_scale`, rather than branching a traced Python bool.
plan-2026-09-02T094601-77d4a04e/D-008 | 2026-09-02 | Ship only the three falsifiable checks in `BridgeConfig.validate()` (patch divisibility, `token_flat_dim == bridge_flat_dim`, `token_seq_len * patches_per_token == num_patches`) and omit a fourth check that no input could ever reach.
plan-2026-09-02T094601-77d4a04e/D-009 | 2026-09-02 | Keep the four SDE variants as pure math objects carrying only their own scalars; the score network is passed as an explicit `score_network=` argument at sampling time rather than stored on the SDE, so `get_config()` stays serializable.
plan-2026-09-02T094601-77d4a04e/D-010 | 2026-09-02 | Ship a local, public `bridge_math_dtype()` helper in `sde.py` rather than centralizing the never-narrow-dtype predicate across the tree's three existing hand-rolled copies, deferring that repo-wide refactor to its own plan.
plan-2026-09-02T094601-77d4a04e/D-025 | 2026-09-02 | Reuse `keras.ops.normalize` for the token decoder, but raise its epsilon to the compute dtype's smallest normal when the ported `1e-12` would underflow to zero (float16), avoiding a silent `0 * inf = nan` on padding rows.
plan-2026-09-02T094601-77d4a04e/D-026 | 2026-09-02 | Ship both upstream GELU formulas as-written (block MLP tanh-approximate, token decoder exact erf) behind a named `GELU_APPROXIMATE = False` constant, pinned by a value-level guard rather than unified.
plan-2026-09-02T094601-77d4a04e/D-033 | 2026-09-02 | Keep the cos-first timestep sinusoidal basis as a private method on `DiTXATimestepEmbedder`, anchored at the `concatenate` call, rather than unifying it with `ScalarSinusoidalEmbedding`'s sin-first basis.
plan-2026-09-02T094601-77d4a04e/D-034 | 2026-09-02 | Apply `cond_mask` strictly after the positional-embedding add, once, upstream of every block, so a masked sample's conditioning stream is the exact zero tensor rather than positions-only.
plan-2026-09-02T094601-77d4a04e/D-035 | 2026-09-02 | Port the conditioning vector as the average of the two timestep embeddings plus the label embedding (`c = (t_emb + t_cond_emb)/2 + y_emb`), not plain DiT's `c = t + y`.
plan-2026-09-02T170923-1285ed83/D-001 | 2026-09-02 | Promote the timestep embedder and the pure-NumPy sin-cos trio to new shared modules `layers/embedding/timestep_embedding.py` / `sincos_pos_embed_2d.py` rather than cross-importing or moving `bit_diffusion`'s module-private copies, accepting a documented, deliberate duplication.
plan-2026-09-02T170923-1285ed83/D-002 | 2026-09-02 | Ship the full upstream DDPM objective (MSE + frozen-out VB term) under stock `fit()` by packing `y_true = concat([noise, x_start, t_plane])`; the loss re-derives `x_t` from its own `DDPMSchedule` rather than dropping the VB term or adding a custom `train_step`.
plan-2026-09-02T170923-1285ed83/D-005 | 2026-09-02 | `SMOKE_PRESET` overrides `variant` and the latent geometry, not just step/epoch counts, deliberately breaking the "smoke changes only how much, never what" rule because DiT-S/2 at 32x32 latents cannot be CPU-wiring-proved in seconds.
plan-2026-09-02T170923-1285ed83/D-010 | 2026-09-02 | `DiffusionConfig.__post_init__` delegates validation to `build_schedule()` and re-raises, rather than hardcoding a `num_timesteps >= 50` floor — measurement showed the real accepted set is `{1} U [20, 25]` at T=25, not a simple threshold.
plan-2026-09-02T170923-1285ed83/D-011 | 2026-09-02 | Reuse `AdaLayerNormContinuous` for `FinalLayer` despite its `scale, shift` chunk order (upstream splits `shift, scale`) — the zero-init kernel and bias make the two orderings equivalent up to relabelling under gradient descent.
plan-2026-09-02T170923-1285ed83/D-012 | 2026-09-02 | Never pass `use_causal_mask` to `DiTBlock`'s stock `MultiHeadAttention` — DiT's attention over image patches is bidirectional, diverging from the `adaln_zero.py` precedent's `use_causal_mask=True` default.
plan-2026-09-02T170923-1285ed83/D-013 | 2026-09-02 | Reproduce upstream's flattened-xavier patch-embed initializer via a duplicated 5-line `flattened_linear_xavier` helper in `dit/model.py`, rather than a cross-package import of `bit_diffusion`'s copy or accepting Keras' wider conv-fan default.
plan-2026-09-02T170923-1285ed83/D-014 | 2026-09-02 | `forward_with_cfg` applies classifier-free guidance to only the first 3 channels (`CFG_GUIDED_CHANNELS = 3`), reproducing upstream's deliberate reproducibility quirk rather than the more "correct"-looking `in_channels` split.
plan-2026-09-02T170923-1285ed83/D-016 | 2026-09-02 | Duplicate the `LEARNED_RANGE` variance interpolation between the loss and the sampler rather than share it across a `models/`<->`losses/` edge, held in lockstep by an executable equality test instead of a comment.
plan-2026-09-02T170923-1285ed83/D-017 | 2026-09-02 | `clip_denoised` defaults to `False`, diverging from upstream's signature default of `True`, to match upstream's actual DiT sampling behaviour on VAE latents that leave `[-1, 1]`.
plan-2026-09-02T170923-1285ed83/D-018 | 2026-09-02 | Replace `keras.backend.standardize_dtype` with `getattr(dtype, "name", None) or str(dtype)` under `models/` (and later `losses/`) to satisfy the repo's whole-tree Keras-2-residue ban.
plan-2026-09-02T170923-1285ed83/D-021 | 2026-09-02 | `src/train/dit/synthetic_data.py` noises through an explicitly UNRESPACED `GaussianDiffusion.q_sample` built from `DiffusionConfig`, never a locally re-typed formula, so the pipeline's `x_t` and the loss's re-derived `x_t` agree structurally rather than coincidentally.
plan-2026-09-02T170923-1285ed83/D-022 | 2026-09-02 | Every data-pipeline entry point takes an explicit `seed` routed through one private `_rng(seed)` branching on `seed is None`, never on truthiness, so `seed=0` reproduces.
plan-2026-09-02T170923-1285ed83/D-023 | 2026-09-02 | `WeightEMACallback` shadows `model.trainable_weights` only, not the frozen `pos_embed` table, because the EMA of a constant is measurably NOT the constant in float32 (drift 1.19e-05 at decay 0.9999 over 200 updates).
plan-2026-09-02T170923-1285ed83/D-024 | 2026-09-02 | The EMA update ships the literal two-term form `decay*shadow + (1-decay)*live`, not the algebraically-identical increment form, because the increment form breaks the `decay=0` exactness contract.
plan-2026-09-02T170923-1285ed83/D-025 | 2026-09-03 | `train_dit.py` builds its optimizer/LR schedule locally via `create_learning_rate_schedule` + `optimizer_builder` instead of `train.common.build_optimizer`, because the shared adapter cannot express upstream's constant `1e-4` learning rate.
plan-2026-09-02T170923-1285ed83/D-028 | 2026-09-03 | The frozen `pos_embed` table follows the model's dtype policy but is floored at float32, never narrowing below it, so a float64 policy no longer silently loses float32-vs-float64 precision.
plan-2026-09-02T170923-1285ed83/D-030 | 2026-09-02 | `DDPMSchedule` stays a shared `utils/` module rather than folded into `dit/diffusion.py` or duplicated per-consumer, because `losses/` and `train/` both need the same tables and `losses/` importing `models/` would invert the dependency direction.
plan-2026-09-03T033750-9bdf25f4/D-002 | 2026-09-03 | Add ONE pure function `statistics_dtype(dtype) -> str` to `utils/dtype_policy.py` (float64 for float64 input, float32 otherwise, raising on non-floating) as the Keras-3 replacement for `keras.backend.result_type`, proven against the live symbol rather than trusting a same-hand oracle.
plan-2026-09-03T033750-9bdf25f4/D-007 | 2026-09-03 | Substitute D-018's `getattr(d, "name", None) or str(d)` idiom inline at all 15 `layers/attention/` `standardize_dtype` sites with no shared helper, and record in the anchor that the idiom is NOT a total-function equivalent — it diverges from the live symbol on `None`, numpy scalar types, and a `keras.DTypePolicy`, safe only because every site passes a tensor's `.dtype`.
plan-2026-09-03T033750-9bdf25f4/D-011 | 2026-09-03 | Replace the import-binding regex with `_import_binds_keras_backend`, which splits the import statement's name list on commas instead of matching position, so `from keras import ops, backend` and its siblings are no longer missed regardless of where `backend` sits in the list.
plan-2026-09-03T085145-3384c4dc/D-001 | 2026-09-03 | Implement `sigsoftmax` in full log space (`w - logsumexp(w)`, `w = z + log_sigmoid(z)`), diverging from the package's own `sparsemax.py` max-shift idiom, because the max-shift form returns `nan nan nan` on an ordinary all-negative logit row (float32 and float16).
plan-2026-09-03T085145-3384c4dc/D-008 | 2026-09-03 | Keep the float16/bfloat16 widening in `_log_sigsoftmax_widened` and guard it with two RED-proven arms, because deleting it returns `nan nan nan` on float16 `[[-40000,-35000,-33000]]` — a category separation, not the precision-only tweak it was first recorded as.
plan-2026-09-03T113223-2a714a91/D-009 | 2026-09-03 | Port the reference's 3-argument `np.meshgrid(grid_h, grid_d, grid_w)` call faithfully in `get_3d_sincos_pos_embed`, with a comment recording why it must not be "simplified" to `indexing='ij'` or three separate calls.
plan-2026-09-03T113223-2a714a91/D-010 | 2026-09-03 | Implement `VideoRoPE3D`'s rotation with the INTERLEAVED pairing the reference's actual math produces (PyTorch/HF `rotate_every_two`), not `Ideogram4MRoPE`'s split-half convention the task prompt's paraphrase claimed.
plan-2026-09-03T113223-2a714a91/D-011 | 2026-09-03 | Implement `LeVJEPABlock` WITHOUT `LayerScale` on either residual branch, matching the pasted PyTorch reference's plain-residual `Block.forward` over `plan.md`'s Step 4 spec.
plan-2026-09-03T113223-2a714a91/D-012 | 2026-09-03 | Implement `_rescale_blocks`'s depth rescaling as a pre-scaled `kernel_initializer` std at construction time instead of a post-build `.assign()`, avoiding the documented `StatelessScope`-discards-`.assign()` trap while remaining distributionally identical.
plan-2026-09-03T113223-2a714a91/D-013 | 2026-09-03 | `LeVJEPAEncoder.__init__` exposes only `use_rope: bool` (no separate `pos_embed=` parameter), matching the reference exactly; RoPE/sincos mutual exclusion is enforced by construction, not a `ValueError` raise.
plan-2026-09-03T113223-2a714a91/D-014 | 2026-09-03 | `LeVJEPAProjector` applies `BatchNormalization(axis=-1)` directly to rank-3 input without the reference's reshape-around-BN workaround, verified numerically identical (max abs diff 0.0) in Keras.
plan-2026-09-03T113223-2a714a91/D-015 | 2026-09-03 | `EMAShadowCallback(decay=...)` accepts either a plain float or a `Callable[[int], float]` schedule, unioning the reference's flat-decay default and this repo's schedule-based `TeacherEMACallback` precedent.
plan-2026-09-03T113223-2a714a91/D-016 | 2026-09-03 | `EMAShadowCallback` does not reproduce the reference's `decay ** elapsed_steps` gap-compensation compounding, since Keras' `on_train_batch_end` has no equivalent step-gap failure mode to compensate for.
plan-2026-09-03T113223-2a714a91/D-017 | 2026-09-03 | `LeVJEPATrainingModel.call`'s `to_float_normalized` is a plain float32 cast, not an invented ImageNet mean/std subtraction never measured against this port's actual data domain.
plan-2026-09-03T113223-2a714a91/D-018 | 2026-09-03 | `LeVJEPATrainingModel.get_config()` serializes `self.encoder` via `serialize_keras_object`/`deserialize_keras_object`, while `projector`/`sigreg` are reconstructed from plain constructor scalars.
plan-2026-09-03T113223-2a714a91/D-019 | 2026-09-03 | The video multi-crop transform replays per-view augmentation across frames via `tf.random.stateless_uniform` keyed on a per-view seed pair, not tensor-result caching, after caching raised `InaccessibleTensorError` under `tf.data`'s `tf.cond` FuncGraph scoping.
plan-2026-09-03T113223-2a714a91/D-020 | 2026-09-03 | The EMA shadow's weights are created with a plain `"zeros"` initializer in `build()` and lazily seeded from the live encoder on `update_ema_shadow`'s first (eager, `on_train_batch_end`) call, after seeding from `build()` raised `NotImplementedError` under `model.fit()`'s tracing context.
plan-2026-09-03T113223-2a714a91/D-023 | 2026-09-03 | `TrainingConfig` defaults to `attn_mode="block_causal"`, `use_rope=True`, `token_drop_rate=0.5` for every run (not just `--smoke`), with CLI flags to opt out, so LeVJEPA's defining mechanisms are reachable by default rather than a flag callers can forget.
plan-2026-09-03T113223-2a714a91/D-024 | 2026-09-03 | Replace a vacuous self-comparison test assertion with an independent-recomputation check (encoder+projector run on the global view alone, compared against the joint forward pass's global row), RED-then-GREEN mutation-proven.
plan-2026-09-05T115518-e69163e4/D-007 | 2026-09-05 | Keep `_POSITIVELY_HOMOGENEOUS_ACTIVATIONS` as a module-private COPY in the new layer rather than importing convunext's frozenset, since `layers/` must never depend upward on `models/`; a warning fires on any activation outside it instead of raising.
plan-2026-09-05T115518-e69163e4/D-009 | 2026-09-05 | `filters` is a REQUIRED constructor argument and the pointwise stage is unconditional; the block has no no-projection mode, leaving that mode served only by calling `create_gabor_depthwise_conv2d` directly.
plan-2026-09-05T115518-e69163e4/D-010 | 2026-09-05 | Follow the HARD golden rule (sub-layers created unconditionally in `__init__`) over the SOFT convention of resolving an initializer inside `build()`, accepting that `get_config()` emits the resolved spec rather than the caller's literal string.
plan-2026-09-05T115518-e69163e4/D-012 | 2026-09-05 | Use `resolve_activation_layer`, not `create_activation_layer`, for the between-stage activation, because the latter was measured to raise on `'linear'` and `'leaky_relu'`, two of the four entries in the positively-homogeneous allowlist.
plan-2026-09-05T115518-e69163e4/D-013 | 2026-09-05 | A-8 falsified: a parent's `trainable=False -> True` transition DOES unfreeze the Gabor sub-layer (Keras 3.8 semantics). Respond with a docstring caveat only, no re-freeze hack in `build()`/`call()`.
plan-2026-09-05T115518-e69163e4/D-017 | 2026-09-05 | Keep the `call()` rank re-assert even though it is unreachable on first invocation; label it rather than delete it or miscount it as tested coverage (resolved early at step 4 once a pre-built-then-recalled path was found to exercise it).
plan-2026-09-05T115518-e69163e4/D-023 | 2026-09-05 | REFLECT verdict NEEDS_WORK on 3 CRITICALs from review pass 1: the layer's headline positive-homogeneity invariant had zero guard, `compute_output_shape`/`build()` had two independently-drifting spatial implementations, and the plan shipped 5 RED tests into the `tests/test_layers/` gate.
plan-2026-09-05T115518-e69163e4/D-026 | 2026-09-05 | The review's prescribed negative homogeneity twin (`pointwise_use_bias=True`) is vacuous as written because Keras zero-initializes a Conv2D bias; ship a twin that assigns a non-zero bias post-build instead. The companion training-mode claim in this entry was later refuted by D-027/D-029/D-032.
plan-2026-09-05T115518-e69163e4/D-027 | 2026-09-05 | D-026's "no external test can redden on the dropped `training=` forward" claim is WRONG, refuted by the repo's own proven-RED `_ContextPoisoner` pattern; the false claim (docstring + anchor) must be deleted from the shipped tree, not merely superseded on paper.
plan-2026-09-05T115518-e69163e4/D-028 | 2026-09-05 | Forward `training=` to `gabor_activation` for symmetry with the other three stages, and qualify the homogeneity docstring/SC-18 with the dtype regime it was measured in (float32/float64), never loosening the bound to cover `mixed_float16`.
plan-2026-09-05T115518-e69163e4/D-029 | 2026-09-05 | Ship only the training-mode guard mechanism proven to redden (the `_ContextPoisoner`, imported not copied); document the other reviewer-proposed mechanism's non-reproduction with its Keras source line so nobody re-attempts it as a guard.
plan-2026-09-05T115518-e69163e4/D-032 | 2026-09-05 | Correct D-030: a direct `block.call(x, training=T/F)` DOES redden under the MB mutation (the public `__call__` path does not); the durable lesson recorded is that an impossibility claim ("no test can", "cannot redden") is the most dangerous sentence in this codebase because it forbids the future work that would refute it.
standalone-2026-09-06-gabor-warm-start/D-001 | 2026-09-06 | `models/vision/convunext/model.py`'s Gabor stem is the paper's TRAINABLE cross-channel `Conv2D` warm start (`create_gabor_conv2d`, Ozbulak & Ekenel SIU 2018), a deliberate reversal of `plan_2026-06-19_ed071c02/D-001`'s frozen depthwise bank; `gabor_filters` becomes the stem's OUTPUT CHANNEL COUNT, the no-projection rule becomes `gabor_filters == initial_filters`, the per-channel front-end is given up, `results/bfconvunext_repro` and `results/bfconvunext_d3` are knowingly broken, and `use_bias=False` stays HARDCODED because positive homogeneity comes from the absence of bias, not from channel summing (measured over 6 inits: 2.54e-07..5.08e-07 bias-free vs 1.75e-01..1.02e+00 with a 0.3 stem bias). NOT an iterative-planner plan: standalone task, no plan directory.
standalone-2026-09-06-gabor-warm-start/D-002 | 2026-09-06 | The identical reversal applied to `models/vision/bias_free_denoisers/bfunet.py`'s independent `_build_gabor_stem`, so the two near-duplicate stem implementations stay in agreement rather than diverging; no archive under `results/` embeds a bfunet-family Gabor stem, so this arm carries zero checkpoint exposure. NOT an iterative-planner plan: standalone task, no plan directory.
plan-2026-09-07T095804-b821967f/D-003 | 2026-09-07 | Do NOT pass `legacy_packages=("dl_techniques.layers.tabular.tabm_blocks",)` when re-registering `RandomSigns` under its new module; rewrite the registration key outright since zero tabm `.keras` archives exist repo-wide and both prior `layers/` move precedents took a clean break.
plan-2026-09-07T095804-b821967f/D-007 | 2026-09-07 | Move `EnsembleInitDistribution` and `_ensemble_scaling_initializer()` (renamed `ensemble_scaling_initializer` by D-010) verbatim into a new private sibling module `layers/tabular/_ensemble_scaling.py`, following the `norms/_masking.py` shared-helper precedent, rather than duplicating either symbol per-file or promoting them to a public `common.py`.
plan-2026-09-07T095804-b821967f/D-009 | 2026-09-07 | Keep `TabMMLPBlock`'s two-attribute activation split (`activation` = serializable value, `activation_fn` = live callable) permanent with a qualified anchor, and narrow the advertised activation surface to a name string or stateless callable by raising `ValueError` on any `keras.layers.Layer` instance (a Layer path silently drops variables from `.keras` archives and crashes when one instance is shared across blocks); also raise `ValueError` when a non-default `ensemble_type` is combined with `k is None`, since `get_config()` would otherwise report a knob the built graph ignores.
plan-2026-09-07T095804-b821967f/D-011 | 2026-09-07 | Keep `LinearEfficientEnsemble.build()`'s conditional `add_weight` calls for `r`/`s`/`bias` strictly conditional on their matching flags (never make any of them unconditional) because an unconditional weight is numerically identical on the forward pass and invisible to every test in the suite, yet silently breaks the `.keras` weight layout for any archive written under the old conditional shape.
plan-2026-09-07T130829-d709705c/D-002 | 2026-09-07 | Apply `clone_initializer(self.scaling_initializer)` at BOTH `add_weight` sites in `LinearEfficientEnsemble` (r and s), per-site rather than two named instances in `__init__`, so the two scaling vectors draw independently instead of sharing one seedless initializer instance.
plan-2026-09-07T130829-d709705c/D-005 | 2026-09-07 | Clone BOTH `kernel_initializer` and `bias_initializer` per block at `TabMBackbone`'s fan-out site (the `self.blocks` comprehension), closing the identical one-instance-fans-out-to-N-`add_weight`-calls defect one composition level above `LinearEfficientEnsemble`, found by a reviewer after the original inventory declared the fix "fully localized".
plan-2026-09-07T130829-d709705c/D-007 | 2026-09-07 | Fix the CLAIM not the mechanism: qualify the `TabMBackbone` kernel/bias-initializer docstrings and the D-005 anchor to "seedless initializers only" — `clone_initializer` deliberately does not break symmetry for a seeded initializer (`initializers/clone.py:71-73`), so `keras.utils.set_random_seed()` is the documented reproducibility idiom, not per-block seed derivation.
plan-2026-09-07T130829-d709705c/D-008 | 2026-09-07 | Apply the D-003 treatment to `ScaleEnsemble.compute_output_shape`: derive `(input_shape[0], self.k, self.input_dim)` from stored config instead of echoing `input_shape`, since the echo was refuted three ways (an unbuilt functional `Input(shape=(None,6))` builds and advertises the wrong static shape; two further built-layer counterexamples) once D-006's rank-3 guard was shown not to make the echo unreachable.
plan-2026-09-07T130829-d709705c/D-009 | 2026-09-07 | Fix the CLAIM not the behaviour a second time: qualify the D-008 anchor and both `compute_output_shape` docstrings to "exact for k>1 (and input_dim>1 for ScaleEnsemble); the exception is the size-1 member axis" rather than assert the derivation is unconditionally correct — the third absolute "cannot happen" claim in this plan to be refuted by measurement.
plan-2026-09-07T130829-d709705c/D-006 | 2026-09-07 | Add an unconditional `len(input_shape) != 3` guard as the first statement of `build()` in the three leaf classes (`ScaleEnsemble`, `LinearEfficientEnsemble`, `NLinear`) only, raising `ValueError`; the two composite classes get no guard of their own and inherit the leaf's raise by delegation, since a naive composite guard would wrongly reject plain (`k is None`) mode's legitimate any-rank `Dense` path.
plan-2026-09-07T161712-985e4d31/D-002 | 2026-09-07 | Clone all SIX `sparse_autoencoder.build()` sites fed by the shared `kernel_initializer`/`bias_initializer` instances (three kernel-fed weights, three bias-fed weights) rather than only the kernel side, and ship both directions of the bias behavior as tests, since the bias fan-out proved live under any caller-supplied randomized `bias_initializer` and inert only at the shipped `'zeros'` default.
plan-2026-09-07T161712-985e4d31/D-003 | 2026-09-07 | Clone `w_spline` and `w_silu` in `kanvolution.build()`; leave `control_points` on the shared instance with an in-source note and a shape-non-coincidence test, since only the rank-4 `w_spline`/`w_silu` pair could ever draw the same values as each other (later refuted for the general rule and corrected by D-009, which clones `control_points` too).
plan-2026-09-07T161712-985e4d31/D-004 | 2026-09-07 | Do not add `clone_initializer` at `TabMModel`'s three fan-out sites (`backbone`, plain `Dense` output, `NLinear` output); a clone there would be a no-op against the measured failure mode since `clone_initializer` preserves seed equality for a caller-supplied SEEDED initializer by contract — document and test the seeded-aliasing caveat instead.
plan-2026-09-07T161712-985e4d31/D-005 | 2026-09-07 | Fix `TabMMLPBlock.compute_output_shape()`'s plain-mode formula to `input_shape[:-1] + (units,)` instead of `(input_shape[0], units)`, since the plain branch is a `keras.layers.Dense` that maps only the last axis; add nothing to `TabMBackbone`, which purely delegates.
plan-2026-09-07T161712-985e4d31/D-008 | 2026-09-07 | Clone BOTH kernel-fed `add_weight` sites in `MPSLayer` (`mps_cores` and `projection`), not just one, after re-measuring that the pair is the same random sample at r ≈ 0.9999999999999968 despite differing ranks/shapes — refuting the shape-matching clearance rule that had exempted this file during EXPLORE.
standalone-2026-09-07-depthwise-gabor-stem/D-001 | 2026-09-07 | `models/vision/convunext/model.py` regains the DEPTHWISE Gabor bank as an OPT-IN second stem arm keyed on the new `gabor_filters_per_channel` (a `depth_multiplier`, built by `create_gabor_depthwise_conv2d`); `None` is the default, so `standalone-2026-09-06-gabor-warm-start/D-001`'s trainable cross-channel `Conv2D` warm start remains THE stem and every existing graph is byte-identical. Not a reversal: what changes is that "the per-channel front-end is given up" is now scoped to the default arm. `gabor_filters` and `gabor_filters_per_channel` are MUTUALLY EXCLUSIVE (exactly one is read; `gabor_filters` keeps its output-channel meaning untouched), the no-projection width rule gains a second form (`input_channels * gabor_filters_per_channel == initial_filters`, textually the retired pre-2026-09-06 rule but against the OTHER count), the bank is built `trainable=True` overriding `create_gabor_depthwise_conv2d`'s own frozen default so `--freeze-gabor-stem` stays the single freeze knob for either stem kind, and `use_gabor_stem=False` with the argument set RAISES rather than silently ignoring it. Homogeneity is unaffected (MEASURED 2.76e-07 on the depthwise arm, inside the Conv2D arm's 2.54e-07..5.08e-07 band); the broken `results/bfconvunext_repro` and `results/bfconvunext_d3` archives are NOT resurrected. NOT an iterative-planner plan: standalone task, no plan directory.
standalone-2026-09-07-depthwise-gabor-stem/D-002 | 2026-09-07 | The trainer arm is ConvUNeXt-ONLY: `--gabor-filters-per-channel` and the `gabor_filters_per_channel` config field live on `train/bfunet/train_convunext_denoiser.py`, NOT in the shared `add_common_arguments` / `BFUnetTrainingConfig`, because `create_bfunet_denoiser` and `create_bfcnn_denoiser` have no such kwarg and a shared flag there could only be inert. `validate_gabor_stem_channels` gains two DEFAULTED keyword parameters (`gabor_filters_per_channel`, `channels`) so its pre-existing three-argument contract — including the refusal of the retired `channels * gabor_filters` rule — is unchanged. `test_the_gabor_stem_channel_rule.py`'s no-private-copy guard was narrowed from a bare substring to a word-boundary regex, because `gabor_filters_per_channel` merely STARTS with `gabor_filters` and its width genuinely does multiply by channels; the guard is strictly stronger on the pattern it targets (it now also catches spacing variants) and carries its own RED proof. NOT an iterative-planner plan: standalone task, no plan directory.
plan-2026-09-07T183458-be1c267e/D-003 | 2026-09-07 | ACCEPT `MultiModalFusion.build()`'s sub-layer-creation-in-`build()` deviation from v2 guide §16.1 (sub-layer COUNT depends on runtime modality count, unknowable pre-`build()`), documented via anchor plus a build-vs-lazy weight-path parity test shipped in the same commit.
plan-2026-09-07T183458-be1c267e/D-004 | 2026-09-07 | Route `graph_neural_network.py`'s `'layer'`/`'batch'` normalization branches through `create_normalization_layer(...)` so all three branches (including the sibling `'rms'` branch) run at the house `1e-6` epsilon instead of Keras' stock `1e-3` taken by accident.
plan-2026-09-07T183458-be1c267e/D-005 | 2026-09-07 | Pass `output_shape=self.concept_dim` to the GAT branch's `MultiHeadAttention`, fixing a silent second shape defect (stock MHA otherwise defaults output width to the query's last axis, not `concept_dim`) that no crash and no existing test could see.
plan-2026-09-07T183458-be1c267e/D-006 | 2026-09-07 | Add a pure private helper `_block_input_shape(node_shape, block_index)` so `build()` and `compute_output_shape` derive per-block shapes from one shared source, fixing `GraphNeuralNetworkLayer.build()` materializing every block at the raw input width and crashing whenever `concept_dim != input width`.
plan-2026-09-07T183458-be1c267e/D-007 | 2026-09-07 | Clone only the 6 of 12 `graph_neural_network.py` fan-out sites MEASURED to actually alias; leave the 3 sites fed by a re-cloning callee (stock `MultiHeadAttention`, `MLPBlock`) uncloned and anchored with their measurement rather than shipping unfalsifiable defensive clones.
plan-2026-09-07T183458-be1c267e/D-008 | 2026-09-07 | Clone `self.kernel_initializer` at all 7 measured-live `MultiHeadLatentAttention.__init__` `Dense` fan-out sites; add no bias guard since the class has no `bias_initializer` parameter and defaults `use_bias=False`.
plan-2026-09-07T183458-be1c267e/D-009 | 2026-09-07 | Clone at all 16 measured-live `MultiModalFusion` fan-out sites (8 kernel + 8 bias) across its 8 mutually-exclusive `_build_*` strategy builders, parametrized per strategy over the modality counts that actually reach each site.
plan-2026-09-07T183458-be1c267e/D-010 | 2026-09-07 | Clone 10 of 14 measured-live `tripse_attention.py` fan-out sites; drop the 4 sites whose callee (`TripletAttentionBranch`, `_SEWeights`, both defined in-file) already clones at its own site, guarding the SE-boundary sites on instance identity rather than a weight value that a later `SqueezeExcitation` commit would move.
plan-2026-09-07T183458-be1c267e/D-011 | 2026-09-07 | Add explicit `name="conv_reduce"`/`name="conv_restore"` to `_SEWeights.build()`, fixing process-order-dependent weight paths (unnamed sub-layers fell back to Keras' global auto-increment counter), with a build-vs-lazy weight-path parity guard in the same commit.
plan-2026-09-07T183458-be1c267e/D-012 | 2026-09-07 | Clone all 4 measured-live `SqueezeExcitation.build()` fan-out sites (2 kernel + 2 bias, corrected from the plan's assumed 2), the plan's widest blast radius at 7 `src/` consumers across 5 test suites; all consumer suites confirmed at or above baseline, closing A-6.
plan-2026-09-07T183458-be1c267e/D-013 | 2026-09-07 | Raise `ValueError` in `GraphNeuralNetworkLayer.__init__` when `concept_dim % num_attention_heads != 0`, enforcing a contract the docstring always claimed but nothing checked (measured: silently floor-divided, wasting channels).
plan-2026-09-07T183458-be1c267e/D-014 | 2026-09-07 | Wire the aggregation-attention branch to `self.num_attention_heads`/`self.concept_dim // self.num_attention_heads` instead of a hardcoded `num_heads=4`, since the constructor argument was dead in the class's own default configuration (`aggregation='attention'`).
plan-2026-09-07T183458-be1c267e/D-015 | 2026-09-07 | Validate `(node_shape, adjacency_shape)` in `GraphNeuralNetworkLayer.build()` (rank, squareness, batch/node-axis agreement, None-safe for symbolic axes), replacing a silent batch-broadcast and an opaque late `call()`-time crash with a named build-time raise.
plan-2026-09-07T183458-be1c267e/D-016 | 2026-09-07 | Raise `ValueError` in `TripletAttentionBranch.__init__` for any `permute_pattern` that is not one of the six permutations of `(0,1,2)`, and correct the module docstring's measured-false blanket validation claim.
plan-2026-09-07T183458-be1c267e/D-017 | 2026-09-07 | Create `MultiHeadLatentAttention`'s dropout sub-layer unconditionally (no `dropout_rate > 0` gate anywhere), dropping the rate gate as unfalsifiable since `keras.layers.Dropout` already short-circuits at rate 0.0 internally.
plan-2026-09-07T183458-be1c267e/D-019 | 2026-09-07 | Correct the D-007 anchor prose to state the MEASURED per-site behaviour (a `MultiHeadAttention`-fed site raises rather than silently staying tied, if the caller's initializer's `get_config()` raises); pass `name="rope"` at MLA's RoPE site; delete `_mla_key`'s disambiguator-stripping, which was hiding 7 independent detections of the deferred RoPE weight-path defect.
plan-2026-09-08T070501-528ded1a/D-002 | 2026-09-08 | Pin `ComplexLayer.kernel_initializer` as documented-dead rather than wiring it up or removing it: no stock `keras.initializers.Initializer` can reproduce the shipped Rayleigh-magnitude / uniform-phase scale at its default, and removing the serialized key would break `from_config` on every existing `.keras` archive, so the knob stays anchored, guarded (AST site count + spy oracle + a determinism-trap test), and inert.
plan-2026-09-08T070501-528ded1a/D-006 | 2026-09-08 | Coerce ANY sequence (not only `tuple`) in `ComplexConv2D`'s and `ComplexAveragePooling2D`'s `kernel_size`/`strides`/`pool_size` normalization, fixing a round-trip-closure defect where a `.keras` save/JSON-load cycle handed `__init__` a `list` instead of a `tuple` and made both classes unloadable from disk.
plan-2026-09-08T111844-de235227/D-007 | 2026-09-08 | Reuse `PixelShuffle2D`/`PixelUnshuffle2D` unchanged despite a MEASURED channel-permutation difference from `torch.nn.PixelShuffle`; absorbed by the learnable per-channel op bracketing every pixel-shuffle site when training from scratch, since `pretrained=True` forecloses weight transfer.
plan-2026-09-08T111844-de235227/D-009 | 2026-09-08 | MDTA's Q/K L2 normalisation uses `stability_floor(self.compute_dtype, 1e-12)` (in-scope `ops.maximum` shape); GDFN has no epsilon and must not acquire one.
plan-2026-09-08T111844-de235227/D-012 | 2026-09-08 | Keep the GDFN factory entry rank-4-only (no rank-3 text-transformer path); MEASURED the transformer grids swallow the resulting exception rather than exercising it, so a dedicated rank-guard test is the real guard.
plan-2026-09-08T111844-de235227/D-013 | 2026-09-08 | GDFN's registry `output_dim_param` is `None`, not `'dim'` — `dim` is the input width the layer expects, not a caller-settable output width.
plan-2026-09-08T111844-de235227/D-015 | 2026-09-08 | Reproduce all six upstream DocRes decoder/encoder asymmetries exactly (depth schedule, no `reduce_chan_level1`, refinement at `2*dim`, unconditional `skip_conv`, no input residual, decoder reuses encoder head counts) rather than "tidying" the decoder into a mirrored U-Net shape.
plan-2026-09-08T111844-de235227/D-016 | 2026-09-08 | `DocRes` refuses non-multiple-of-8 spatial extents loudly (in both `build()` and `call()`) instead of auto-padding, because padding cannot be silently removed from the prediction without telling the caller.
plan-2026-09-08T111844-de235227/D-017 | 2026-09-08 | Prove the D-007 pixel-shuffle "successor" bracketing claim with a recorded forward pass (`forward_order_oracle.py`) rather than re-typing the op order or grepping source text, upgrading the adjacency check to real data-flow.
plan-2026-09-08T111844-de235227/D-018 | 2026-09-08 | Replace the plan-specified "perturb input by a constant" no-residual guard (MEASURED non-falsifiable — DocRes is not shift-invariant) with an exact `atol=0` identity check against `output_conv`'s recorded output.
plan-2026-09-08T111844-de235227/D-019 | 2026-09-08 | Ship the DTSPrompt generators on numpy+scipy only; six of seven primitives are bit-exact against OpenCV/scikit-image, the one exception (bilinear resize) uses a hand-written `_resize_bilinear` rather than `scipy.ndimage.zoom`'s align-corners default.
plan-2026-09-08T111844-de235227/D-020 | 2026-09-08 | Use the 15-bit BGR2GRAY luma triple `(3735, 19235, 9798)`, not the widely-quoted 14-bit one, which is measurably wrong against OpenCV 4.13.0 by one grey level.
plan-2026-09-08T111844-de235227/D-021 | 2026-09-08 | The base coordinate grid normalises each axis independently (channel 0 = x/width, channel 1 = y/height) rather than collapsing to one scalar divisor, so it is defined correctly on non-square pages.
plan-2026-09-08T111844-de235227/D-022 | 2026-09-08 | `TASKS`' `loss`/`postprocess` fields are validated NAMES, not eager `keras`/callable objects, keeping the table framework-free for both the offline sidecar precompute and the inference shim.
plan-2026-09-08T111844-de235227/D-023 | 2026-09-08 | Dispatch archive extraction by format (`zipfile` for `.zip`, `libarchive-c` for everything else) so all 11 DIBCO/H-DIBCO years are extractable; `libarchive-c` is deliberately left undeclared in `pyproject.toml` and degrades to a named error.
plan-2026-09-08T111844-de235227/D-024 | 2026-09-08 | Prove staging completeness by decoding every archive member (not trusting `Content-Length` or a checksum, neither of which the DIBCO/UCI hosts provide) and writing the `.ok` marker last.
plan-2026-09-08T111844-de235227/D-025 | 2026-09-08 | RDD and DIR300 are wired as `manual_drive` slots and never partially downloaded — Google Drive folder pagination caps bulk listing at ~51 of 300 files, and a silently-truncated corpus is worse than an honest empty one.
plan-2026-09-08T111844-de235227/D-028 | 2026-09-08 | Decode training triplets with PIL inside one `tf.numpy_function`, not `tf.io.decode_image`, because the staged binarization corpus is full of TIFF that the native decoder cannot read.
plan-2026-09-08T111844-de235227/D-029 | 2026-09-08 | Pair an input page to its ground truth by stem key AND pixel size, not stem key alone — NoisyOffice ships three same-stem targets at different resolutions under one input.
plan-2026-09-08T111844-de235227/D-030 | 2026-09-08 | Key inference input preparation off `spec.postprocess`, not a task-string branch, accepting that deblurring shares the `CLAMP_IMAGE` mode's behaviour with deshadowing/appearance (amended by D-033).
plan-2026-09-08T111844-de235227/D-032 | 2026-09-08 | Correct the false docstring claim about upstream's ink-class polarity but KEEP `BINARY_INK_CLASS_INDEX = 1` (upstream is class 0) — the divergence is provably internal and flipping it would invalidate the only measured end-to-end checkpoint.
plan-2026-09-08T111844-de235227/D-033 | 2026-09-08 | Add `TaskSpec.max_input_size` so deblurring is no longer forced through the >=1600px homomorphic illumination re-composition it never had upstream, restoring upstream's always-pad deblurring behaviour on its common path.
plan-2026-09-08T111844-de235227/D-034 | 2026-09-08 | Refuse dewarping's `.npy` float32 prompt sidecars loudly (`UnsupportedPromptDtypeError`) at both pipeline entry points rather than shipping an incomplete decode arm that would repair only 1 of 3 defects and make the other 2 look supported.
plan-2026-09-09T042752-6d66ac56/D-004 | 2026-09-09 | New layers (RoutingModule/ChunkLayer/DeChunkLayer) live in a new `layers/dynamic_chunking/` subpackage, curated `__init__.py`, deliberately NO factory and NO registry entry — a one-consumer registry is dispatch machinery with no payoff.
plan-2026-09-09T042752-6d66ac56/D-005 | 2026-09-09 | The `t`/`T` mixer reuses `group_query` attention (MHA special case) with an externally built rank-3 causal(+band) mask, accepting its INTERLEAVED RoPE pairing as a recorded, deliberate divergence from the reference's split-half (no reference checkpoints are ever loaded, so the pairing does not bind).
plan-2026-09-09T042752-6d66ac56/D-007 | 2026-09-09 | Port the padded/masked path only (drop `cu_seqlens`); replace the reference's data-dependent `max(boundary_mask.sum(-1))` width with a fixed `max_chunks` constructor argument, truncating in POSITION order to avoid the magnitude-order causality defect this repo already measured once.
plan-2026-09-09T042752-6d66ac56/D-010 | 2026-09-09 | All six step-2 primitive-probe questions answered by executed commands with no falsification signal: argsort/graph-safety/EMA-recurrence/RoPE-liveness/causal-mask/SwiGLU-sizing all confirmed as planned.
plan-2026-09-09T042752-6d66ac56/D-012 | 2026-09-09 | `RoutingModule` shipped with a locally derived `10D+1` float32 tolerance and both step-3 oracle corrections (boundary predicate `p > 0.5`, non-identity-projection parity arm) applied.
plan-2026-09-09T042752-6d66ac56/D-013 | 2026-09-09 | `ChunkLayer` shipped with an exact `atol=0.0` bound derived from a zero-rounded-operation count, the `max_chunks` cap anchored to D-007, and a `max_chunks > L` right-padding rule the plan text did not specify.
plan-2026-09-09T042752-6d66ac56/D-014 | 2026-09-09 | `DeChunkLayer` shipped with `keras.ops.while_loop` (D-010c), a one-hot masked accumulator plus `maximum_iterations` (both forced by measured `slice_update`/`scan` backward-pass limitations on this backend) and a scale-aware derived `atol`.
plan-2026-09-09T042752-6d66ac56/D-015 | 2026-09-09 | Curated `__all__ = ["ChunkLayer", "DeChunkLayer", "RoutingModule"]`, D-004's no-factory/no-registry ruling placed as the anchor in the `__init__.py` docstring itself.
plan-2026-09-09T042752-6d66ac56/D-016 | 2026-09-09 | `hnet/config.py` ships a Keras-free recursive `arch_layout` parser plus a frozen-dataclass data model, with the depth-scaled-init denominator choice (flat vs per-stage cumulative) deliberately DEFERRED to step 11.
plan-2026-09-09T042752-6d66ac56/D-017 | 2026-09-09 | Isotropic stack ships as three public builders (`build_mixer`/`build_mlp`/`build_causal_keep_mask`) plus two registered layers; RMSNorm epsilon, Mamba-2 norm knobs and SwiGLU sizing are all passed EXPLICITLY at every call site rather than relying on defaults.
plan-2026-09-09T042752-6d66ac56/D-018 | 2026-09-09 | The straight-through gate is one named module-level function with exactly ONE grouped expression (`x + stop_gradient(hard - x)`), anchored to forbid reassociation, because STE reassociation is forward-invisible (the STE literally returns 1.0) and only a bit-identity float64 arm can see it move.
plan-2026-09-09T042752-6d66ac56/D-019 | 2026-09-09 | `residual_proj` ships `kernel_initializer="zeros"` with Keras' default random `bias_initializer` left as `"zeros"` too (not the reference's random bias) — a recorded divergence, not a fidelity claim.
plan-2026-09-09T042752-6d66ac56/D-020 | 2026-09-09 | `HNetStage` deliberately does NOT implement `compute_output_shape` — supplying one would type the two boolean mask outputs as float32 under Keras 3's shape-only symbolic path.
plan-2026-09-09T042752-6d66ac56/D-021 | 2026-09-09 | The depth-scaled init divides by `n_residuals_by_stage()` (the reference's outside-in CUMULATIVE per-stage count), not the single hierarchy-wide `n_residuals()` — the two coincide only at the innermost stage, which is why a 1-stage guard cannot see the difference at all.
plan-2026-09-09T042752-6d66ac56/D-025 | 2026-09-09 | FineWeb-Edu staging is gated by an explicit `--download` flag checked before disk/mkdir/network, with `--dry-run` overriding `--download` rather than being independent of it — `allow_download = not args.dry_run` was rejected as a naming-convention trap.
plan-2026-09-09T042752-6d66ac56/D-026 | 2026-09-09 | `HNetTrainingConfig` omits a per-stage LR-multiplier field (Keras 3's optimizer carries one LR for all variables; the reference's own shipped configs carry no multiplier values either) and fixes a real `decay_steps` horizon bug the schedule guard caught pre-ship (5.6x overshoot with no shape symptom).
plan-2026-09-09T042752-6d66ac56/D-029 | 2026-09-09 | Both step-18 product defects repaired IN THE LAYER via one new shared `indexing.py` module (`pad_permutation_to_width`, `batched_gather`) rather than hand-copying the padding block — the copy would have reproduced the exact two-file lockstep invariant that caused the asymmetry defect.
plan-2026-09-09T042752-6d66ac56/D-030 | 2026-09-09 | GPU tolerance bounds now take `max(CPU float32 derivation, 4 * MEASURED matmul unit roundoff)` instead of trusting `tensor_float_32_execution_enabled()`, which returns `True` even on a CPU-only process — the 3 REFLECT GPU failures were TF32 rounding, not a code defect.
plan-2026-09-09T042752-6d66ac56/D-031 | 2026-09-09 | `d_intermediate` wired end-to-end (all 16 shipped SwiGLU widths measured before/after, diff empty); the scale-ceiling extrapolation from D-009's single-width Mamba probe is WITHDRAWN rather than adjusted, because D-028 measured the real dev model ~30x faster than that reasoning predicted.
plan-2026-09-09T042752-6d66ac56/D-032 | 2026-09-09 | `infer_hnet.py`'s sampler/temperature/window were already correct; the pass-2 findings against them were all SURVIVING MUTATIONS hidden by a two-source blind spot (a fixture uniform across the tested axis AND a checkpoint uniform across it too), not code defects — fixed by asserting fixture discrimination as the guard's first arm.
plan-2026-09-09T042752-6d66ac56/D-033 | 2026-09-09 | `GaborDepthwiseSeparableBlock`'s inherited repo-wide serialization RED is a REAL defect (unhashable-dict activation config reaching a frozenset membership test) and worse than the guard's own description predicted; fixed at step 19, closing the repo-root sweep to 0 failed.
plan-2026-09-11T050223-1b47bcf6/D-004 | 2026-09-11 | Implement `NormalConsistencyLoss`/`LocalConsistencyLoss` as finite-difference cross-product / discrete-Laplacian approximations instead of a PCA/eigendecomposition-based normal estimator, since the latter is disproportionately expensive and harder to unit-test against hand-derived toy tensors.
plan-2026-09-11T050223-1b47bcf6/D-006 | 2026-09-11 | Derive a per-image pinhole `K` from a fixed assumed horizontal FOV constant plus the image's own width/height, since neither KITTI nor MegaDepth carries usable camera intrinsics locally.
plan-2026-09-11T050223-1b47bcf6/D-010 | 2026-09-11 | `MaskHead` always fixes `DPTDecoder`'s `output_activation` to `"linear"`, never `"sigmoid"`, to match `MaskLoss`'s `BinaryCrossentropy(from_logits=True)` contract without double-squashing the gradient.
plan-2026-09-11T050223-1b47bcf6/D-011 | 2026-09-11 | Both dense heads run with `upsample_factor=1`, emitting outputs at the encoder's native patch-grid resolution rather than full pixel resolution, since `patch_size=14` is not a power of 2 that `DPTDecoder`'s upsample stages can restore.
plan-2026-09-11T050223-1b47bcf6/D-013 | 2026-09-11 | Conditioning signals fuse as extra image channels concatenated onto RGB before the unmodified ViT encoder, and the two state-indicator embeddings add to the encoder's own output tokens afterward, rather than reimplementing patch embedding to reach token-level fusion earlier.
plan-2026-09-11T050223-1b47bcf6/D-014 | 2026-09-11 | `StateIndicatorEmbedding.build()` constructs a SECOND, independently-instantiated initializer for `absent_embedding` rather than reusing the same initializer object passed to `present_embedding`, since one Keras 3 initializer instance called twice with the same shape returns bit-identical draws.
plan-2026-09-11T050223-1b47bcf6/D-015 | 2026-09-11 | `SparseDepthSplat` and `ConditioningInputEncoder` eagerly force-build every sublayer in their own `build()`, including ones a given runtime branch (e.g. absent-modality) would otherwise skip, closing a Keras-3 unbuilt-state gap under `filterwarnings=error`.
plan-2026-09-11T050223-1b47bcf6/D-016 | 2026-09-11 | `y_true` is the documented 5-tuple `(gt_ray, gt_distance, gt_point, gt_mask, valid_mask)` with the model's own output reduced to the matching 4 slots as `y_pred`; `gt_distance` is derived from stored planar depth via `radial = planar / ray_z`, never passed through as if it were already radial.
plan-2026-09-11T050223-1b47bcf6/D-017 | 2026-09-11 | `MaskLoss`'s ground truth reuses each loader's own `valid_mask` (measurability, not semantics) as a pragmatic zero-new-data substitute for the paper's unavailable semantic sky/foreground mask.
plan-2026-09-11T050223-1b47bcf6/D-020 | 2026-09-11 | A training-only `OmniPointTrainingWrapper` reports `OmniPointCombinedLoss` via `self.add_loss()` instead of `model.compile(loss=...)`, since Keras 3.8's `CompileLoss` broadcasts one loss callable independently per tuple-output slot rather than calling it once over the whole tuple, which would break the loss's shared-`s_star`-computed-once invariant.
plan-2026-09-11T050223-1b47bcf6/D-021 | 2026-09-11 | `OmniPointTrainingWrapper` downsamples every `y_true` element to the heads' own coarser patch-grid resolution via nearest-neighbor only, never bilinear/average, to preserve the unit-norm ray and binary-mask invariants that a blended downsample would silently violate.
plan-2026-09-11T050223-1b47bcf6/D-022 | 2026-09-11 | The RGB/depth/mask scatter-reprojection helper stays local to `generate_synthetic_camera_eval_set.py` rather than being promoted into `camera_models.py`, since it is a raster-resampling concern (not ray geometry) with only two total call sites.
plan-2026-09-11T050223-1b47bcf6/D-023 | 2026-09-11 | `OmniPoint.build()` force-builds all three heads (and, per its completion-fix extension, the two conditioning layers) via a dummy forward pass, closing a `.keras` reload weight-loss gap caused by Keras 3's lazy-build-at-first-`call()` machinery never running during `build_from_config()`.
plan-2026-09-11T050223-1b47bcf6/D-026 | 2026-09-11 | `OmniPointCombinedLoss`'s `s_star` is now derived from `gt_ray * pred_distance`, never `pred_ray * pred_distance`, restoring the paper's literal formula and making `L_point`/`s_star` genuinely invariant to a wrong predicted ray.
plan-2026-09-11T050223-1b47bcf6/D-028 | 2026-09-11 | `OmniPointTrainingWrapper` now wires the dataset's own `gt_ray` into the model as intrinsics conditioning during training, giving the previously dead `--enable-conditioning` branch real gradient (sparse-depth conditioning explicitly left unwired, out of this fix's scope).
plan-2026-09-11T050223-1b47bcf6/D-029 | 2026-09-11 | `intrinsics_present` is now drawn per-sample from a Bernoulli(0.9) via a layer-held `SeedGenerator`, fixing D-028's own regression where hardwiring it to all-True leaked the literal `L_ray` supervision target into the model as input and permanently killed `absent_embedding`'s gradient.
plan-2026-09-11T143552-7e7a2ca8/D-002 | 2026-09-11 | Before `exec_module` runs, pop `Custom>{name}` for each of the 5 legacy-alias-bearing pinned-module class names out of the LIVE `keras.saving.get_custom_objects()` dict (never out of `snapshot`), so `register_dl_technique` sees no existing claimant and the pre-existing post-`exec_module` restore already repairs full state.
plan-2026-09-11T160509-2e1049ac/D-001 | 2026-09-11 | Fix DiT's `TestJitCompileAgreesWithEager` by opting into the shared `tf32_disabled` fixture first (falsified as unreachable from `tests/test_models/`), falling back to the pre-planned `max(reassociation_atol(...), matmul_precision_atol(scale))` composition already established in `test_gated_linear_attention_block.py` and `test_hnet/test_components.py`.
plan-2026-09-12T075714-035fd488/D-001 | 2026-09-12 | Build a new `LoRAAdapter` layer indexed by a global occurrence counter (round-robin over `num_mem_blocks` physical blocks), applied only to the shared MLP block's up-projection, not to the shared attention block's projections.
plan-2026-09-12T075714-035fd488/D-005 | 2026-09-12 | `Zamba2MambaBlock` instances are built fresh (independently weighted) at every 'm' position; `Zamba2SharedAttentionBlock`/`Zamba2SharedMLPBlock` instances are built exactly `num_mem_blocks` times total and reused round-robin across all 'g' positions.
plan-2026-09-12T075714-035fd488/D-006 | 2026-09-12 | Built a local `Zamba2TrainingConfig`/`build_datasets` in `src/train/zamba2/common.py` calling the leaf dataset helpers directly, rather than subclassing `train.common.clm_pretrain.ClmPretrainConfig`, because its `load_train_val_datasets` unconditionally wraps labels for a dict-output model and `Zamba2Model.call` returns a plain tensor.
plan-2026-09-12T075714-035fd488/D-007 | 2026-09-12 | `LoRAAdapter.build()`'s `_a_initializer` now calls `clone_initializer(a_initializer_fn)` fresh inside the per-occurrence loop, since one resolved seedless initializer instance replays the identical draw at every call of the same shape, making every occurrence's A slice bit-identical pre-fix.
plan-2026-09-12T075714-035fd488/D-008 | 2026-09-12 | Wrapped the module-level `MODEL_VARIANTS` table in `types.MappingProxyType(...)` so the class-level alias `Zamba2Model.MODEL_VARIANTS = MODEL_VARIANTS` no longer resolves to a flagged mutable-default binding under the repo-wide guard.
plan-2026-09-12T075714-035fd488/D-009 | 2026-09-12 | Split `MODEL_VARIANTS` into `num_mem_blocks` (physical, forwarded to the constructor) and a new table-only `num_mem_block_occurrences` (popped by `from_variant`, fed to `_build_layer_mapping`), since `from_variant` had been passing the physical block count as the occurrence count, making every shipped variant invoke each physical mem-block exactly once.
plan-2026-09-12T123331-28fd855f/D-002 | 2026-09-12 | Place the shared tied-embedding-logits helper as a single plain function `tied_embedding_logits()` in a new `src/dl_techniques/utils/tied_embeddings.py`, consolidating only the matmul/transpose/cast/bias expression across 5 call sites, not the differing "build lm_head only if untied" control flow; `fftnet` moves to `models/vision/fftnet/` as a plain leaf package.
plan-2026-09-12T123331-28fd855f/D-004 | 2026-09-12 | Cast the tied-embedding matmul RESULT (not either operand) to float32 unconditionally at all 5 call sites, matching the standard Keras mixed-precision convention of keeping loss-facing logits in float32 regardless of upstream compute dtype.
plan-2026-09-12T123331-28fd855f/D-005 | 2026-09-12 | Changed the float32 cast to a floor semantic (promote only when narrower than float32, never touch float32-or-wider) and registered `gpt2`/`wave_field` in `precision_arm_subjects.py` with `expected_compute_dtype="float32"`, following the repo's `ideogram4` documented-pin precedent; extended consolidation to `cliffordnet/lm.py` and `nano_vlm/model.py`.
plan-2026-09-12T123331-28fd855f/D-006 | 2026-09-12 | Inverted the `dtype_exempt_outputs` index on the `gpt2`/`wave_field` precision-arm pins from 1 (`last_hidden_state`, a real activation) to 0 (`logits`, the intentional float32 output), and added a mutation-probe regression guard proving the guard now correctly goes RED when `last_hidden_state` is cast to float32.
plan-2026-09-12T173329-e20362c4/D-004 | 2026-09-12 | `Mamba2` is a pure encoder with no baked LM head and no `hidden_size` attribute (only `d_model`); do NOT wire `masked_language_model.clm.CausalLanguageModel` onto it (its internal dict-input causal shift would double-shift against the packed-CLM pipeline's own pre-shift) — instead `build_causal_lm_model` is a small local functional wrapper reusing only the shared `tied_embedding_logits` matmul helper.
plan-2026-09-12T195532-422091c3/D-005 | 2026-09-12 | Introduce one private helper (`_backbone_forward`) inside `CausalLanguageModel` that centralizes the "run the backbone, then extract logits-or-hidden-state depending on `skip_head`" branch, called from all 3 sites (`call`, `_apply_output_head`, the causality probe) at its introduction.
plan-2026-09-12T195532-422091c3/D-007 | 2026-09-12 | Add an optional `loss_fn: Optional[keras.losses.Loss] = None` constructor parameter to `CausalLanguageModel`; when provided, `compute_loss` delegates entirely to it (a single call), preserving gemma/qwen's existing `--loss-type focal`/`--label-smoothing` support that a hardcoded-CE wrap would have silently regressed.
plan-2026-09-12T195532-422091c3/D-008 | 2026-09-12 | Fixed a `skip_head=False` lazy-build-in-traced-`train_step` crash with a one-line eager dummy-forward call in `mamba/common.py`'s `build_model` (scoped to mamba, not `clm.py`); reported but did not fix `Mamba2`'s embedding-lookup name mismatch (`self.embedding`) making `tie_word_embeddings` inert post-migration.
plan-2026-09-12T195532-422091c3/D-009 | 2026-09-13 | Added `get_embedding_matrix()` to `Mamba2` (returns `self.embedding.embeddings`), matching `CausalLanguageModel._locate_embedding_weights`'s first lookup check and restoring genuine weight tying, per explicit user authorization that no checkpoint compatibility risk applies.
plan-2026-09-13T052422-19022ba2/D-002 | 2026-09-13 | Add `aggregate_backbone_losses: bool = False` to `CausalLanguageModel`; when set and `self.backbone.losses` is non-empty, `train_step`/`test_step` add `ops.sum(self.backbone.losses)` to the computed loss, letting a backbone's own `add_loss` term (HNet's ratio loss) train live under the shared wrapper.
plan-2026-09-13T052422-19022ba2/D-003 | 2026-09-13 | Add `causality_probe_plain_tensor: bool = False` to `CausalLanguageModel`; when `True` the causality probe passes a bare tensor (no dict, no attention_mask) to `_backbone_forward`, since Zamba2Model/HNet accept only a plain positional tensor and the probe's default dict-call previously failed silently inside a broad try/except.
plan-2026-09-13T052422-19022ba2/D-004 | 2026-09-13 | Folded the pre-existing `Zamba2TrainingConfig` REGISTERED-list gap fix into the zamba2 migration step (same file already open for review) rather than deferring to a separate follow-up plan.
plan-2026-09-13T052422-19022ba2/D-008 | 2026-09-13 | Pass `causality_tolerance=1e-5` (not the default 0.0) when constructing `CausalLanguageModel` for WaveFieldLLM, calibrated between a measured clean-config noise floor (1.19e-06) and a measured documented-leak value (4.53e-04) under the same probe methodology, so `verify_causality=True` still catches a genuine leak while tolerating WaveFieldAttention's irreducible FFT float32 noise floor.
plan-2026-09-13T052422-19022ba2/D-010 | 2026-09-13 | Left `loss_fn` unset when constructing `CausalLanguageModel` for HNet (HNetTrainingConfig has no loss_type/focal_gamma/label_smoothing fields, unlike gpt2/zamba2/wave_field), so `compute_loss` falls through to the same hardcoded from-logits sparse-crossentropy HNet used pre-migration, verified numerically unchanged.
plan-2026-09-13T052422-19022ba2/D-011 | 2026-09-13 | Relocated HNet's mandated eager dummy-forward call from `build_model()` into `train()` (the real training entry point), because placing it in `build_model()` would force real weight allocation and two causality-probe passes through every one of the six large reference variants, silently breaking `test_the_choice_builds_a_model`'s documented ~2.6s/no-device-memory cost contract; added `assert not model.built` to guard against regression.
plan-2026-09-13T073704-245ab5d5/D-010 | 2026-09-13 | `pretrain_so.py`'s SO (soft-orthonormal) penalty was confirmed (by bit-identical-to-control gradient measurement) to never reach `train_step`'s gradient tape; fixed with a second `tf.GradientTape` scoped to `so_loss` and a second `apply_gradients` call inside the existing instance-level `train_step` monkeypatch, proven RED-before/GREEN-after by a mutation-sensitive test.
plan-2026-09-13T073704-245ab5d5/D-012 | 2026-09-13 | Ported `hidden_size` (alias to `d_model`) and `get_embedding_matrix()` onto `Mamba` (v1), verbatim-verified against `Mamba2`'s own mechanism (both store `self.embedding` as a bare `keras.layers.Embedding`) after reading both files in full, not assumed from the plan text.
plan-2026-09-13T073704-245ab5d5/D-013 | 2026-09-13 | `SymmetricInfoNCELoss` is a two-view/in-batch-negatives loss (not listwise/grouped, contra both plan-anticipated paths); built `Qwen3EmbeddingTowerPair` encoding query/positive as `view_a`/`view_b` and cloned `SimCSEModel`'s proven stacked-batch + stock-`fit()` + `jit_compile=False` shape rather than either originally-named fallback.
plan-2026-09-13T120637-288bad33/D-012 | 2026-09-13 | Added a scannable `# DECISION` anchor adjacent to hnet's SUPERSEDED D-008 prose (never reworded) so validate-plan.mjs's anchor scan can find it.
plan-2026-09-13T120637-288bad33/D-013 | 2026-09-13 | Added a scannable `# DECISION` anchor adjacent to the reranker seed-pin prose in test_synthetic_separation_improves.py, summarizing D-011's seed-robustness follow-up.
plan-2026-09-13T120637-288bad33/D-014 | 2026-09-13 | Retracted old D-015's ambiguous "redundant" framing; documented test_run_artifacts.py and test_finetune_checkpoint_load.py as permanently complementary via cross-referencing anchors in both files.
plan-2026-09-13T120637-288bad33/D-016 | 2026-09-13 | Softened D-011's root-cause claim (epoch budget, not architecture, is the leading untested confound) via its own dedicated anchor block.
plan-2026-09-13T165751-bc5433cb/D-001 | 2026-09-14 | Wrap `MambaLayer._selective_scan`'s call site (v1) and `Mamba2Layer._ssm_scan`'s call site (v2) in `tf.recompute_grad`, guarded by a `keras.backend.backend() == "tensorflow"` check, trading recompute FLOPs for memory to address zero gradient checkpointing on the scan; the in-code comment at both wrap sites is written as the D-005 anchor since this decision's own effect could only be honestly anchored once measured.
plan-2026-09-13T165751-bc5433cb/D-003 | 2026-09-14 | Replace `keras.ops.convert_to_tensor(self.D)` with `tf.convert_to_tensor(self.D)` in `MambaLayer.call()`'s scan call site, because `keras.ops.convert_to_tensor` is a no-op for anything `tf.is_tensor()`-true (including a raw `tf.Variable`), so the bare `self.D` Variable was still crossing the `tf.recompute_grad` boundary unmaterialized and doubling its own gradient (D-002).
plan-2026-09-13T165751-bc5433cb/D-005 | 2026-09-14 | Final iteration-1 measured outcome, documented at both wrap call sites: v1's ceiling improved batch=2->4 at seq_len=128 (12GB GPU, `130m` variant); v2's ceiling stayed at batch=1 (per-layer cost ~4x v1's); the original batch=8/seq=128 target was not reached for either, a disclosed partial result rather than a hidden shortfall.

## Retired anchors

plan-2026-09-13T165751-bc5433cb/D-002 | RETIRED 2026-09-14 | Its `# DECISION` comment lived at the `self.D` scan-call argument in `MambaLayer.call()` (`src/dl_techniques/models/language/mamba/components.py`), documenting the MEASURED 2.0x gradient-doubling defect on `self.D` under `tf.recompute_grad`. It was superseded and removed at EXECUTE step 2.1 when the actual fix landed as D-003 (`tf.convert_to_tensor(self.D)`, not the no-op `keras.ops.convert_to_tensor(self.D)` first attempted) — the comment at that call site now reads D-003 and explicitly says it supersedes D-002. No `plans/ANCHORS.md` retirement record was written at the time because this file did not exist yet (this plan had not closed); written retroactively here at this plan's own CLOSE, per the plain-prose note left at the top of this plan's `decisions.md` requesting exactly this remediation.

plan-2026-09-14T042205-a11f6af3/D-003 | 2026-09-14 | Ship the chunked `_selective_scan` (per-timestep `deltaA`/`deltaB_u` computation inside the `while_loop` body, replacing the full-sequence precompute) for v1 — bit-exact forward numerics, gradient-correctness re-verified, additive to the existing `tf.recompute_grad` wrap; measured outcome batch=4->5 (batch=8 not reached), v2's `d_state` reduction named as a deferred follow-up.
plan-2026-09-14T042205-a11f6af3/D-005 | 2026-09-14 | Fixed a vacuous per-weight gradient-correctness test (`reassociation_atol`'s `max(1.0, scale)` floor swamped `A_log`'s near-zero gradient) by normalizing both gradients by their own `scale` before comparing and evaluating the tolerance at `scale=1.0`, making the check genuinely scale-relative instead of absolute-floored; RED-proved against the reviewer's own mutation.
plan-2026-09-14T165315-47f9d575/D-001 | 2026-09-14 | Split `standard_blocks.py` into 5 single-class files (ConvBlock/BasicBlock/BottleneckBlock to `layers/conv_blocks/`, DenseBlock/ResidualDenseBlock to `layers/` root), each with its own per-module registration string and no compat shim at the old path — anchored at CLOSE (`layers/AGENTS.md:39`) after the adversarial review found this plan had shipped zero in-tree anchors for the ruling.
plan-2026-09-15T034909-a7edc8da/D-002 | 2026-09-15 | Host the unified activation-spec helper in `utils/activation_serialization.py` with a new `allow_layer: bool = True` parameter (default preserves ~60 existing callers), and convert `layers/activations/common.py`'s trio into thin wrappers around it rather than deleting it, so its distinct import path and Layer-rejecting default survive without forcing every current importer to change.
plan-2026-09-15T034909-a7edc8da/D-003 | 2026-09-15 | Ship a PARTIAL (not full) delegation in `common.py`'s trio: `resolve_activation`/`activation_spec` keep str/dict resolution inline and delegate only the Layer-rejection raise, because MEASURED full delegation would have returned a non-callable string for a bare-string input and raised on unregistered custom-activation dicts that the inline `keras.activations.deserialize` path resolves successfully.
plan-2026-09-15T034909-a7edc8da/D-006 | 2026-09-15 | Keep `clifford_rnn.py`'s pre-existing D-010 anchor verbatim and append a SUPERSEDED (source, not policy) note beside it when repointing the import from `clifford_block.py`'s private trio to `layers/activations/common.py`: D-010's "do not fork a new shared module" ruling is unaffected since `common.py` already existed for independent reasons, not invented for this migration.
plan-2026-09-15T034909-a7edc8da/D-008 | 2026-09-15 | Exempt `bias_free_conv2d.py` from the activation-helper migration: its `get_config`/`from_config` deliberately round-trips a `keras.layers.Layer`-instance activation, a contract `common.py`'s Layer-rejecting `resolve_activation` cannot serve; documented with a cross-reference comment, no functional change.
plan-2026-09-15T034909-a7edc8da/D-009 | 2026-09-15 | Exempt `convunext/model.py::_make_supervision_activation` from migration for the same reason as D-008 (it exists specifically to clone a Layer-instance activation via `keras.layers.serialize`/`deserialize`, incompatible with `common.py`'s Layer-reject contract); documented with a cross-reference comment, no functional change.
plan-2026-09-15T034909-a7edc8da/D-011 | 2026-09-15 | Fixed a real latent bug in `multimodal_fusion.py`: `from_config` unconditionally deserialized via `keras.activations.deserialize`, which silently returns a bare class-name STRING (not a reconstructed Layer) for a Layer-shaped serialized dict — added a `dict`/module-tag type guard directly rather than migrating to `common.py`'s trio, since this class's forward pass genuinely uses Layer-instance activations today and migrating would be a breaking change, not a superset.
plan-2026-09-15T034909-a7edc8da/D-012 | 2026-09-15 | Left `gated_mlp.py::_get_activation`'s narrow 5-item allow-list unmigrated: a separate, earlier `valid_activations` check in `__init__` already rejects anything outside the five names before `_get_activation` ever runs, so migrating only `_get_activation` (as literally instructed) would be inert dead-code widening with no observable effect — documented with comments at both surfaces.
plan-2026-09-15T034909-a7edc8da/D-014 | 2026-09-15 | Restored `common.py::resolve_activation`'s final-branch `callable()` validation, lost during the Step 2 migration, which had let non-callable garbage (int/float/bool/list) construct successfully and fail later with a confusing `TypeError` inside `call()` instead of an immediate `ValueError` at construction time; RED-proven regression test added.
plan-2026-09-15T034909-a7edc8da/D-015 | 2026-09-15 | Restored `film.py`'s `gamma_activation`/`beta_activation` public-attribute type (raw spec, not the resolved callable the Step 4e migration had silently substituted) by splitting into a public spec attribute plus a private resolved-callable attribute, mirroring `clifford_block.py`'s established two-attribute pattern.
plan-2026-09-15T094955-31fbe3db/D-004 | 2026-09-15 | Migrated `bias_free_conv2d.py` (Idiom B) fully onto `serialize_activation`/`deserialize_activation`, deleting its `from_config` overrides; left `tabm_mlp_block.py` (Idiom E) as a narrow, evidence-backed exception because `allow_layer=False`'s built-in error message/traceback-frame identity would break two test-pinned observables the round-trip measurement never covered.
plan-2026-09-15T094955-31fbe3db/D-005 | 2026-09-15 | Removed `multimodal_fusion.py`'s hand-rolled module-tag Layer-dict check (Idiom C), replacing it with direct `deserialize_activation`/`serialize_activation` calls, gated GO by a measurement confirming the shared pair correctly dispatches this file's exact Layer-shaped dict.
plan-2026-09-15T094955-31fbe3db/D-006 | 2026-09-15 | Added a clarifying trailing comment to `convunext/model.py::_make_supervision_activation`'s existing D-009 exception documentation, explaining it stays permanent because it clones a live Layer under a new name (not a stored-spec round trip), so composing it through `serialize_activation`/`deserialize_activation` would add indirection with no behavior change.
plan-2026-09-15T094955-31fbe3db/D-008 | 2026-09-15 | Fixed `shgcn/model.py` and `fastvlm/model.py` to resolve their activation constructor argument eagerly in `__init__` rather than deferring to `get_config()`/save time, restoring construction-time Layer-rejection (Invariant 1); updated two test assertions that had pinned the pre-fix raw-string storage as an implementation detail.
plan-2026-09-15T094955-31fbe3db/D-010 | 2026-09-15 | Restored `multimodal_fusion.py`'s two-way Layer-dict/function-dict dispatch in `from_config` after an earlier same-plan fix (D-005) over-generalized to a single call and regressed the exception type for an unregistered custom-function activation dict from `ValueError` to a confusing `TypeError`; independently re-measured and narrowed the adversarial review's own causal claim before fixing.
plan-2026-09-15T094955-31fbe3db/D-011 | 2026-09-15 | Migrated `bias_free_conv1d.py` to parity with its already-fixed 2D sibling (deferred-resolution defect, never in original scope) and restored `bias_free_conv2d.py`'s `activation=None -> 'linear'` serialization contract, a silent regression the earlier D-004 migration introduced.
plan-2026-09-15T135450-e083ae85/D-001 | 2026-09-15 | Keep tabm_mlp_block.py's hand-rolled Layer-rejection ValueError rather than migrating onto deserialize_activation(allow_layer=False); strengthened the existing anchor with the explicit two-blocker (message-token + traceback-frame) rationale so a future reader does not re-propose the migration.
plan-2026-09-15T135450-e083ae85/D-003 | 2026-09-15 | Leave multimodal_fusion.py __init__'s raw keras.activations.get(activation) unchanged (it is the only expression that both resolves a string and passes a Layer through unchanged); documented both canonical helpers' specific shortfalls at the call site.
plan-2026-09-15T135450-e083ae85/D-004 | 2026-09-15 | Fixed multimodal_fusion.py's from_config Layer-vs-function dict dispatch to a structural predicate (isinstance(config.get('config'), dict)) instead of a module-string check, closing a real misroute for any custom (non-keras.layers) Layer-subclass activation.
plan-2026-09-15T135450-e083ae85/D-005 | 2026-09-15 | Migrated vit_siglip/model.py to eager __init__-time activation resolution via resolve_activation, deleting the now-redundant from_config override, mirroring the shgcn/fastvlm D-008 shape.
plan-2026-09-15T135450-e083ae85/D-006 | 2026-09-15 | Migrated vit_hmlp/model.py identically to D-005, as its own independently-tested step.
plan-2026-09-15T135450-e083ae85/D-013 | 2026-09-15 | Added regularizers.get(...) resolution in __init__ for kernel_regularizer/bias_regularizer in both vit_hmlp/model.py and vit_siglip/model.py, fixing a real regression the D-005/D-006 from_config-deletion migration introduced (sublayers were unaffected; the model's own public attribute held a raw dict).
plan-2026-09-15T135450-e083ae85/D-016 | 2026-09-15 | Clarified via a new adjacent anchor that D-013's "restoring pre-migration behavior" framing is accurate for vit_hmlp (a step-4b regression) but not for vit_siglip (a pre-existing defect predating this plan and step 4a); D-013 left untouched per append-only convention.
plan-2026-09-16T052349-7dfede94/D-002 | 2026-09-16 | Ship `output_mode='normalized'` as RBFProtoNet's ONLY supported RBF-head mode (not a togglable knob defaulting to `'basis'`), matching RBFLayer's own documented recommendation for trainability.
plan-2026-09-16T052349-7dfede94/D-003 | 2026-09-16 | Trainer uses `SparseCategoricalCrossentropy(from_logits=False)` against the RBF head's already-softmax-normalized output, deliberately deviating from the repo's `from_logits=True` convention to avoid silently double-applying softmax.
plan-2026-09-16T052349-7dfede94/D-006 | 2026-09-16 | Standalone smoke test measured `create_mixture_layer('rbf', units=100, output_mode='normalized')` composes cleanly at `feature_dim=128` in both float32 and mixed_float16 (looser fp16 sum-to-1 tolerance only); locked `feature_dim=128` as the backbone's pooled output dimension for Step 2 onward.
plan-2026-09-16T052349-7dfede94/D-008 | 2026-09-16 | Fixed `train_rbf_protonet.py`'s `create_callbacks` to thread `run_dir` into `create_common_callbacks`, closing a run-directory-splitting bug where checkpoints/logs landed in an orphan sibling dir instead of alongside `config.json`.
plan-2026-09-16T064227-b83f88ad/D-002 | 2026-09-16 | Extract `_create_rbf_head(num_classes, repulsion_strength, min_distance, name="rbf_head")` as a module-level function in `model.py`, shared by `RBFProtoNet._build_rbf_head` and `CliffordRBFProtoNet._build_rbf_head`; no shared backbone base class introduced.
plan-2026-09-16T064227-b83f88ad/D-005 | 2026-09-16 | Confirmed via standalone smoke probe that the isotropic composition (patch_size=2 stem + 12 CliffordNetBlocks at channels=128 + GlobalAveragePooling2D) produces the expected (B, 128) output, no NaN/Inf, and live (non-None, non-zero) gradients on all 160 trainable weights; locked this exact stem/block/StochasticDepth composition for the model class.
plan-2026-09-16T085926-41908265/D-001 | 2026-09-16 | Fixed CliffordCLIP's stale registration key `dl_techniques.models.clip.clifford_clip` to convention-correct `dl_techniques.models.clifford_clip.model`; user-approved, no existing `.keras` checkpoints reference the old key.
plan-2026-09-16T114148-70556a67/D-005 | 2026-09-16 | Fixed train_clip.py's validate_model_loading call site to build `expected` via .predict() instead of a bare __call__, so the round-trip check compares matched eval paths (the reported max_diff=0.002095 was an XLA-vs-eager accumulation-order artifact, not checkpoint weight loss).
plan-2026-09-16T114148-70556a67/D-008 | 2026-09-16 | Added a CliffordCLIP-trainer-local fallback in train_clip.py that writes a minimal training_log.csv (header + summary row) at train-end when the shared StepCheckpointCallback's dynamic-schema mode never flushed one (interval-gated below log-every-steps).
plan-2026-09-17T032714-403de954/D-003 | 2026-09-17 | Hard-raise in TrainingConfig.__post_init__ on any non-zero label_smoothing, changed the field's own default (and --label-smoothing's CLI default) from 0.1 to 0.0, and removed the now-unreachable warning-and-ignore branch, since SparseCategoricalCrossentropy has no label_smoothing parameter and the sparse-label pipeline has no one-hot path.
plan-2026-09-17T032714-403de954/D-006 | 2026-09-17 | Fixed train_vit.py's create_callbacks to accept and thread the already-created run_dir into create_common_callbacks(run_dir=...), eliminating a doubled vit_vit_..._<ts1>_<ts2> sibling results directory that every training invocation previously produced; also deduplicated viz_dir onto the helper's own returned results_dir.
plan-2026-09-17T064004-0d194df2/D-001 | 2026-09-17 | Hand-rolled train_kan.py's run-directory/callback wiring using run_io.prepare_run_dir/default_experiment_name/save_training_history_json plus best_checkpoint_path() and directly-constructed ModelCheckpoint/CSVLogger, instead of adopting create_callbacks() even with monitor/include_analyzer overrides, to avoid reopening the prior plan's already-closed EarlyStopping/--patience question.
plan-2026-09-17T064004-0d194df2/D-002 | 2026-09-17 | Sequenced visualization work: Iteration 1 ships only output-directory unification for the existing post-hoc plugins; Iteration 2 adds a per-epoch KANVisualizationCallback mirroring DenoisingVisualizationCallback's shape, sharing one rendering helper with the final post-hoc plot_results() rather than duplicating grid-construction logic.
